FILE: android\build.gradle.kts
================================================================================

allprojects {
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
    }
}

val newBuildDir: Directory =
    rootProject.layout.buildDirectory
        .dir("../../build")
        .get()
rootProject.layout.buildDirectory.value(newBuildDir)

subprojects {
    val newSubprojectBuildDir: Directory = newBuildDir.dir(project.name)
    project.layout.buildDirectory.value(newSubprojectBuildDir)
}
subprojects {
    project.evaluationDependsOn(":app")
}

tasks.register<Delete>("clean") {
    delete(rootProject.layout.buildDirectory)
}


================================================================================
FILE: android\settings.gradle.kts
================================================================================

pluginManagement {
    val flutterSdkPath = run {
        val properties = java.util.Properties()
        file("local.properties").inputStream().use { properties.load(it) }
        val path = properties.getProperty("flutter.sdk")
        require(path != null) { "flutter.sdk not set in local.properties" }
        path
    }

    includeBuild("$flutterSdkPath/packages/flutter_tools/gradle")

    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
        gradlePluginPortal()
    }
}

plugins {
    id("dev.flutter.flutter-plugin-loader") version "1.0.0"
    id("com.android.application") version "8.11.1" apply false
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android") version "2.1.0" apply false
    id("com.google.gms.google-services") version "4.4.2" apply false
}

include(":app")

================================================================================
FILE: lib\add_food_post_page.dart

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show kIsWeb;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'dart:convert';
import 'notification_service.dart';
import 'models/food_analysis_model.dart';
import 'services/ai_food_service.dart';
import 'services/ai_moderation_service.dart';
import 'services/ai_priority_engine.dart';
import 'widgets/food_analysis_card.dart';
import 'core/app_config.dart';

class AddFoodPostPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;

  const AddFoodPostPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<AddFoodPostPage> createState() => _AddFoodPostPageState();
}

class _AddFoodPostPageState extends State<AddFoodPostPage> {
  final ImagePicker _picker = ImagePicker();

  final TextEditingController titleCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController descCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController qtyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController locationCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController pickupCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController expiryCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController startTimeCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController endTimeCtrl = TextEditingController();

  File? selectedImage;
  Uint8List? webImage;
  bool isUploading = false;

  // ─── AI scan state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  bool _isAiScanning = false;
  FoodAnalysisResult? _aiResult;

  String get cloudName => AppConfig.cloudinaryCloudName;
  String get uploadPreset => AppConfig.cloudinaryUploadPreset;

  // PHASE 2 FIX: Category field so ReceiverHomePage filter chips work
  String selectedCategory = "Meals";
  final List<String> categories = ["Meals", "Vegetables", "Bakery", "Dairy", "Other"];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    titleCtrl.dispose();
    descCtrl.dispose();
    qtyCtrl.dispose();
    locationCtrl.dispose();
    pickupCtrl.dispose();
    expiryCtrl.dispose();
    startTimeCtrl.dispose();
    endTimeCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Image pick + AI trigger
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Future<void> _pickImage() async {
    final XFile? file = await _picker.pickImage(
      source: ImageSource.gallery,
      imageQuality: 70,
      maxWidth: 1080,
      maxHeight: 1080,
    );
    if (file == null) return;

    Uint8List? bytes;
    File? fileRef;

    if (kIsWeb) {
      bytes = await file.readAsBytes();
      webImage = bytes;
    } else {
      fileRef = File(file.path);
      selectedImage = fileRef;
    }

    // Reset previous AI result
    setState(() {
      _aiResult = null;
      _isAiScanning = true;
    });

    // Trigger AI scan — always pass bytes (v3 API is bytes-only)
    Uint8List? scanBytes = bytes;
    if (scanBytes == null && fileRef != null) {
      scanBytes = await fileRef.readAsBytes();
    }
    if (scanBytes != null) _runAiScan(imageBytes: scanBytes);
  }

  Future<void> _runAiScan({required Uint8List imageBytes}) async {
    if (!AppConfig.hasGeminiKey) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isAiScanning = false);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('AI scan unavailable: set GEMINI_API_KEY when building the app.'),
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    try {
      final result = await AiFoodService.analyze(
        imageBytes: imageBytes,
      );

      if (!mounted) return;

      setState(() {
        _aiResult = result;
        _isAiScanning = false;
      });

      // Auto-sync category from AI detection
      _syncCategoryFromAi(result.foodCategory);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _isAiScanning = false;
        // Leave _aiResult = null → scan failed gracefully
      });
      _snack('AI scan failed: please check your image and try again.');
    }
  }

  /// Optionally align the dropdown category with AI's food category detection
  void _syncCategoryFromAi(FoodCategory aiCategory) {
    const map = {
      FoodCategory.vegetables: 'Vegetables',
      FoodCategory.fruits: 'Vegetables', // closest match
      FoodCategory.bakery: 'Bakery',
      FoodCategory.dairy: 'Dairy',
      FoodCategory.cookedMeals: 'Meals',
      FoodCategory.meat: 'Meals',
      FoodCategory.beverages: 'Other',
      FoodCategory.other: 'Other',
    };
    final mapped = map[aiCategory];
    if (mapped != null && categories.contains(mapped)) {
      setState(() => selectedCategory = mapped);
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Time pickers
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Future<void> _pickTime({required bool isStart}) async {
    final TimeOfDay? picked = await showTimePicker(
      context: context,
      initialTime: TimeOfDay.now(),
    );
    if (picked != null && mounted) {
      final formatted = picked.format(context);
      setState(() {
        if (isStart) {
          startTimeCtrl.text = formatted;
        } else {
          endTimeCtrl.text = formatted;
        }
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickExpiryRangeToday() async {
    await _pickTime(isStart: true);
    await _pickTime(isStart: false);
    if (startTimeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty && endTimeCtrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
      expiryCtrl.text = "Today from ${startTimeCtrl.text} to ${endTimeCtrl.text}";
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Validation
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  String? _validate() {
    if (titleCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return "Please enter food title.";
    if (descCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return "Please enter description.";
    if (qtyCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return "Please enter quantity.";
    if (locationCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return "Please enter pickup location.";
    final q = int.tryParse(qtyCtrl.text.trim());
    if (q == null || q <= 0) return "Quantity must be a valid number (> 0).";
    if (expiryCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return "Please select expiry time.";
    if (selectedImage == null && webImage == null) return "Please select an image.";
    if (_isAiScanning) return "Please wait for AI scan to complete.";
    return null;
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Blocking / warning logic before submit
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Returns false if submission should be blocked.
  /// In production (hardBlockUnsafe=true), unsafe food CANNOT be submitted.
  Future<bool> _checkAiBeforeSubmit() async {
    if (_aiResult == null) return true; // No scan → allow (graceful fallback)

    // ── HARD BLOCK: unsafe food or moderation failure ──────────────────────
    if (_aiResult!.isUnsafe) {
      // Production mode: permanent block, no bypass
      await _showHardBlockDialog();
      return false;
    }

    // ── NON-BLOCKING WARNING: expiring soon / low confidence ───────────────
    if (_aiResult!.requiresWarning && !_aiResult!.isUnsafe) {
      await _showWarningDialog();
    }

    return true;
  }

  Future<void> _showHardBlockDialog() async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.block_rounded, color: Color(0xFFE74C3C), size: 52),
        title: const Text(
          'Upload Blocked',
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF991B1B)),
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text(
              _aiResult!.safetyRecommendation,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, height: 1.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFFFEF2F2),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: const Text(
                'Our AI system has flagged this food as unsafe for donation. '  
                'Donating spoiled or unsafe food endangers recipients. '  
                'This upload has been blocked to protect the community.',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Color(0xFF991B1B), height: 1.5),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFE74C3C),
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Understood', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }


  Future<void> _showWarningDialog() async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: Color(0xFFF39C12), size: 40),
        title: const Text('Food Quality Notice', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: Text(
          '${_aiResult!.safetyRecommendation}\n\nPlease ensure the food meets safety standards before donating.',
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, height: 1.5),
        ),
        actions: [
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: Colors.green,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Understood', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Cloudinary upload
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Future<String?> _uploadImageToCloudinary() async {
    if (selectedImage == null && webImage == null) return null;

    try {
      late Uint8List imageBytes;

      if (kIsWeb && webImage != null) {
        imageBytes = webImage!;
      } else if (selectedImage != null) {
        imageBytes = await selectedImage!.readAsBytes();
      }

      final uri = Uri.parse("https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/$cloudName/image/upload");
      var request = http.MultipartRequest('POST', uri);
      request.fields['upload_preset'] = uploadPreset;
      request.files.add(
        http.MultipartFile.fromBytes(
          'file',
          imageBytes,
          filename: 'image.jpg',
        ),
      );

      var responseStream = await request.send();
      var response = await http.Response.fromStream(responseStream);

      if (response.statusCode != 200) {
        final errorData = json.decode(response.body);
        final errorMessage = errorData['error']?['message'] ?? 'Unknown error';
        throw Exception("Cloudinary: $errorMessage");
      }

      final data = json.decode(response.body);
      return data['secure_url'];
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text("Image upload failed: $e"), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
        );
      }
      return null;
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Phase 2: Background moderation check
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Runs moderation in the background — does not block the UX.
  /// Writes results (fraudRiskScore, moderationFlags) back to the post.
  void _runModerationCheck(String postId, {required Uint8List? imageBytes}) {
    if (imageBytes == null) return;
    AiModerationService.checkPost(
      imageBytes: imageBytes,
      donorId: widget.userId,
      title: titleCtrl.text.trim(),
    ).then((result) {
      AiModerationService.persistModerationResult(
        postId: postId,
        result: result,
      );
    }).catchError((_) {});
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Submit
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final String? err = _validate();
    if (err != null) {
      _snack(err);
      return;
    }

    // AI blocking / warning gate
    final bool proceed = await _checkAiBeforeSubmit();
    if (!proceed) return;

    setState(() => isUploading = true);

    try {
      final String? imageUrl = await _uploadImageToCloudinary();
      if (imageUrl == null) {
        setState(() => isUploading = false);
        return;
      }

      final userDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(widget.userId).get();
      final String dName = userDoc.data()?['name'] ?? "Donor";
      final String dAvatar = userDoc.data()?['avatarUrl'] ?? "";

      // Build base post data
      final Map<String, dynamic> postData = {
        "userID": widget.userId,
        "donorName": dName,
        "donorAvatar": dAvatar,
        "title": titleCtrl.text.trim(),
        "description": descCtrl.text.trim(),
        "quantity": int.parse(qtyCtrl.text.trim()),
        "location": locationCtrl.text.trim(),
        "pickupInstructions": pickupCtrl.text.trim(),
        "expiryDate": expiryCtrl.text.trim(),
        "imageUrl": imageUrl,
        "status": "active",
        // PHASE 2 FIX: Write category so receivers can filter by it
        "category": selectedCategory,
        "createdAt": FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      };

      // Merge AI analysis fields if scan was performed
      if (_aiResult != null) {
        postData.addAll(_aiResult!.toFirestore());

        // ── Phase 2: Food quality score ──────────────────────────────────
        postData['foodQualityScore'] =
            AiPriorityEngine.computeFoodQualityScore(postData);

        // ── Phase 2: Delivery time prediction ───────────────────────────
        final deliveryPrediction = AiPriorityEngine.predictDelivery(
          remainingHours: _aiResult!.estimatedRemainingHours,
          urgencyLevel: _aiResult!.urgencyLevel.name,
        );
        postData.addAll(deliveryPrediction);
      }

      final postRef = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("posts").add(postData);

      // ── Phase 2: Moderation check (async, post-upload) ─────────────────
      _runModerationCheck(postRef.id, imageBytes: webImage ?? await selectedImage?.readAsBytes());

      final int quantity = int.parse(qtyCtrl.text.trim());
      final bool isAiUrgent = _aiResult != null &&
          (_aiResult!.urgencyLevel.name == 'urgent' ||
           _aiResult!.urgencyLevel.name == 'critical');

      // Notify Receivers always
      List<String> rolesToNotify = ["Receiver"];

      // NGO auto-escalation:
      //  • large donation (qty ≥ 50)  OR
      //  • AI marks it as urgent/critical regardless of quantity
      if (quantity >= 50 || isAiUrgent) {
        rolesToNotify.add("NGO");
      }

      final String notifTitle = isAiUrgent
          ? '⚡ Urgent Food Donation!'
          : 'New Food Available!';
      final String notifMessage = isAiUrgent
          ? "URGENT: '${titleCtrl.text.trim()}' needs pickup soon — AI flagged it as expiring."
          : "A new donation '${titleCtrl.text.trim()}' is available near you.";

      final usersSnap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection("users")
          .where("role", whereIn: rolesToNotify)
          .get();
      for (var doc in usersSnap.docs) {
        if (doc.id != widget.userId) {
          await NotificationService.sendNotification(
            recipientId: doc.id,
            title: notifTitle,
            message: notifMessage,
            type: isAiUrgent ? "urgent_post" : "post",
            relatedId: postRef.id,
          );
        }
      }

      if (!mounted) return;

      showDialog(
        context: context,
        builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_rounded, color: Colors.green, size: 48),
          title: const Text("Posted!", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          content: const Text("Your food post has been created successfully."),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                Navigator.pop(context);
              },
              child: const Text("OK"),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      _snack("Upload failed: $e");
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => isUploading = false);
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Helpers
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  void _snack(String msg) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(msg),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 10),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _field({
    required String label,
    required IconData icon,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    bool readOnly = false,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
    int maxLines = 1,
    TextInputType? keyboardType,
  }) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xffF4F6FA),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: TextField(
        controller: controller,
        readOnly: readOnly,
        onTap: onTap,
        maxLines: maxLines,
        keyboardType: keyboardType,
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          border: InputBorder.none,
          labelText: label,
          labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xff7D8FAB)),
          prefixIcon: Icon(icon, color: Colors.green),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildImagePreview() {
    if (!kIsWeb && selectedImage != null) {
      return ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Image.file(selectedImage!, fit: BoxFit.cover, width: double.infinity, height: 170),
      );
    } else if (kIsWeb && webImage != null) {
      return ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Image.memory(webImage!, fit: BoxFit.cover, width: double.infinity, height: 170),
      );
    } else {
      return Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: const [
          Icon(Icons.photo_camera, color: Colors.green, size: 34),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text("Upload Photo", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          Text("Tap to choose from gallery", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
        ],
      );
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // AI section widget
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Widget _buildAiSection() {
    // Nothing selected yet → hide
    if (selectedImage == null && webImage == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        Row(
          children: const [
            Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, color: Colors.green, size: 18),
            SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              "AI Freshness Analysis",
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 15),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        if (_isAiScanning) const FoodAnalysisLoadingCard(),
        if (!_isAiScanning && _aiResult != null)
          FoodAnalysisCard(result: _aiResult!),
        if (!_isAiScanning && _aiResult == null) ...[
          // Scan failed or not yet run
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFFFFF8E1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFFFD54F)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: const [
                Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: Color(0xFFF57F17), size: 20),
                SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'AI scan unavailable. Please verify food freshness manually before posting.',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Color(0xFF5D4037), height: 1.4),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Build
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isUnsafe = _aiResult != null && _aiResult!.isUnsafe;
    final bool submitEnabled = !isUploading && !_isAiScanning && !isUnsafe;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF5F7F6),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        title: const Text(
          "Add New Food Post",
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black),
        ),
        leading: const BackButton(color: Colors.black),
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // ── Photo picker ──────────────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Add Photos", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              GestureDetector(
                onTap: _pickImage,
                child: Container(
                  height: 170,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade400),
                  ),
                  child: _buildImagePreview(),
                ),
              ),

              // ── AI Analysis Card (auto-appears after image pick) ──────
              _buildAiSection(),

              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Food Item Name ────────────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Food Item Name", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              _field(label: "e.g., Vegetable soup", icon: Icons.fastfood, controller: titleCtrl),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Description ───────────────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Description", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              _field(
                label: "Ingredients / allergens (if any)",
                icon: Icons.notes,
                controller: descCtrl,
                maxLines: 4,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Quantity + Expiry ─────────────────────────────────────
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Quantity", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        _field(
                          label: "e.g., 5",
                          icon: Icons.format_list_numbered,
                          controller: qtyCtrl,
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Expiry Time (Today)", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 6),
                        _field(
                          label: "Select time range",
                          icon: Icons.access_time,
                          controller: expiryCtrl,
                          readOnly: true,
                          onTap: _pickExpiryRangeToday,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Category ──────────────────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Category", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              // PHASE 2 FIX: Category dropdown to enable receiver filtering
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xffF4F6FA),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                  child: DropdownButton<String>(
                    value: selectedCategory,
                    isExpanded: true,
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down, color: Colors.green),
                    items: categories.map((cat) {
                      return DropdownMenuItem(value: cat, child: Text(cat));
                    }).toList(),
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      if (val != null) setState(() => selectedCategory = val);
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Location ──────────────────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Pickup Location", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              _field(label: "Enter address / area", icon: Icons.location_on, controller: locationCtrl),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),

              // ── Pickup Instructions ───────────────────────────────────
              const Text("Pickup Instructions (Optional)", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              _field(label: "e.g., ring bell; leave at gate", icon: Icons.tips_and_updates, controller: pickupCtrl, maxLines: 3),
              const SizedBox(height: 26),

              // ── Unsafe Block Warning ────────────────────────────────
              if (isUnsafe) ...[
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0xFFFEF2F2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFFCA5A5)),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.block, color: Color(0xFFEF4444), size: 24),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          "This food is not safe for donation.\n${_aiResult!.safetyRecommendation}",
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFB91C1C), fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ],

              // ── Submit button ─────────────────────────────────────────
              Tooltip(
                message: isUnsafe ? "Blocked: Food is not safe for donation" : "",
                child: SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 54,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: submitEnabled ? Colors.green : Colors.grey.shade400,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    ),
                    onPressed: submitEnabled ? _submit : null,
                    child: isUploading
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 24,
                            height: 24,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 3),
                          )
                        : _isAiScanning
                            ? Row(
                                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                children: const [
                                  SizedBox(
                                    width: 16,
                                    height: 16,
                                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                                  ),
                                  SizedBox(width: 10),
                                  Text("AI Scanning…", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                ],
                              )
                            : const Text(
                                "Post Food",
                                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'notifications_page.dart';
import 'add_food_post_page.dart';
import 'my_food_posts_page.dart';
import 'incoming_requests_page.dart';
import 'donor_profile_page.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';

class DonorDashboardPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const DonorDashboardPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<DonorDashboardPage> createState() => _DonorDashboardPageState();
}

class _DonorDashboardPageState extends State<DonorDashboardPage> {
  bool isLoading = true;
  String userName = "User";
  int familiesHelped = 0;
  List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> recentActivity = [];
  int selectedIndex = 0;
  StreamSubscription<Position>? _positionSubscription;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    loadDashboardData();
    _startLocationTracking();
  }

  void _startLocationTracking() async {
    bool hasPermission = await _handleLocationPermission();
    if (hasPermission) {
      _positionSubscription = Geolocator.getPositionStream(
        locationSettings: const LocationSettings(
          accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high,
          distanceFilter: 10,
        ),
      ).listen((Position position) {
        FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(widget.userId).update({
          "lastLocation": GeoPoint(position.latitude, position.longitude),
          "heading": position.heading,
        });
      });
    }
  }

  Future<bool> _handleLocationPermission() async {
    bool serviceEnabled;
    LocationPermission permission;

    serviceEnabled = await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled();
    if (!serviceEnabled) return false;

    permission = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
    if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
      permission = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
      if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) return false;
    }
    if (permission == LocationPermission.deniedForever) return false;
    return true;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _positionSubscription?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> loadDashboardData() async {
    try {
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
      final currentUser = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
      if (currentUser == null) return;

      final userDoc = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(widget.userId);
      final userSnap = await userDoc.get();

      if (userSnap.exists) {
        final data = userSnap.data();
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            userName = data?["name"] ?? "User";
            familiesHelped = data?["familiesHelped"] ?? 0;
          });
        }
      } else {
        await userDoc.set({
          "name": "User",
          "email": currentUser.email ?? "",
          "role": "Donor",
          "familiesHelped": 0,
          "createdAt": FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      }

      final recentSnap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection("posts")
          .where("userID", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .limit(10)
          .get();

      final recentDocs = recentSnap.docs..sort((a, b) {
        final aTime = (a.data() as Map)['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
        final bTime = (b.data() as Map)['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
        if (aTime == null || bTime == null) return 0;
        return bTime.compareTo(aTime);
      });

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          recentActivity = recentDocs.take(3).toList();
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("❌ Dashboard load error: $e");
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Widget _getPage(int index) {
    switch (index) {
      case 0: return _dashboardBody();
      case 1: return MyFoodPostsPage(userId: widget.userId);
      case 2: return IncomingRequestsPage(userId: widget.userId);
      case 3: return DonorProfilePage(userId: widget.userId);
      default: return _dashboardBody();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF8FAFC),
      body: _getPage(selectedIndex),
      bottomNavigationBar: _modernBottomNav(),
    );
  }

  Widget _dashboardBody() {
    return CustomScrollView(
      slivers: [
        SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildHeader()),
        SliverPadding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 24),
          sliver: SliverList(
            delegate: SliverChildListDelegate([
              _buildHeroActionCard(),
              const SizedBox(height: 30),
              _buildStatsRow(),
              const SizedBox(height: 30),
              _buildAnalyticsSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 35),
              const Text(
                "Recent Activity",
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF1F2937)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              _buildRecentActivityList(),
              const SizedBox(height: 40),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAnalyticsSection() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const Text(
          "Donation Analytics",
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF1F2937)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Row(
          children: [
            _buildAnalyticsCard("Sales", Icons.bar_chart, Colors.blue, () => _showSalesDetail()),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            _buildAnalyticsCard("Orders", Icons.shopping_cart, Colors.orange, () => _showOrdersDetail()),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            _buildAnalyticsCard("Customers", Icons.people, Colors.purple, () => _showCustomersDetail()),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAnalyticsCard(String title, IconData icon, Color color, VoidCallback onTap) {
    return Expanded(
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: color.withOpacity(0.05),
                blurRadius: 10,
                offset: const Offset(0, 4),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Icon(icon, color: color, size: 32),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14)),
              const Text("View Detailed", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: Colors.grey)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showSalesDetail() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => _AnalyticsDetailSheet(
        title: "Sales (Completed Donations)",
        color: Colors.blue,
        child: _buildSalesContent(),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showOrdersDetail() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => _AnalyticsDetailSheet(
        title: "Orders (Total Requests)",
        color: Colors.orange,
        child: _buildOrdersContent(),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showCustomersDetail() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => _AnalyticsDetailSheet(
        title: "Customers (Receivers)",
        color: Colors.purple,
        child: _buildCustomersContent(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSalesContent() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('posts')
          .where('userID', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .where('status', whereIn: ['completed', 'delivered'])
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (!snapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        final docs = snapshot.data!.docs;

        // Simple Chart Logic: count per day for last 7 days
        Map<String, int> dailyCounts = {};
        final now = DateTime.now();
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
          final date = now.subtract(Duration(days: i));
          final key = "${date.day}/${date.month}";
          dailyCounts[key] = 0;
        }

        for (var doc in docs) {
          final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
          final ts = data['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
          if (ts != null) {
            final date = ts.toDate();
            final key = "${date.day}/${date.month}";
            if (dailyCounts.containsKey(key)) {
              dailyCounts[key] = dailyCounts[key]! + 1;
            }
          }
        }

        final sortedKeys = dailyCounts.keys.toList().reversed.toList();
        final maxCount = dailyCounts.values.fold(1, (max, v) => v > max ? v : max);

        return Column(
          children: [
            // Simple Bar Chart
            Container(
              height: 150,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 10),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: sortedKeys.map((key) {
                  final count = dailyCounts[key]!;
                  final heightFactor = count / maxCount;
                  return Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        width: 25,
                        height: 100 * heightFactor + 5,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.blue.shade400,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(key, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: Colors.grey)),
                    ],
                  );
                }).toList(),
              ),
            ),
            const Divider(),
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: Text("Completed Donations", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
              ),
            ),
            ...docs.map((doc) {
              final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              return ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.blue),
                title: Text(data['title'] ?? "Donation"),
                subtitle: Text(data['description'] ?? "", maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                trailing: Text((data['createdAt'] as Timestamp?)?.toDate().toString().split(' ')[0] ?? ""),
              );
            }),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOrdersContent() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('requests')
          .where('donorId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (!snapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        final docs = snapshot.data!.docs;

        if (docs.isEmpty) return const Center(child: Text("No orders found."));

        return ListView.builder(
          shrinkWrap: true,
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          itemCount: docs.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final data = docs[index].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
            final status = data['status'] ?? 'pending';
            return ListTile(
              leading: Icon(Icons.shopping_bag, color: status == 'accepted' ? Colors.green : Colors.orange),
              title: Text(data['itemTitle'] ?? "Request"),
              subtitle: Text("From: ${data['receiverName'] ?? 'Unknown'}"),
              trailing: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: (status == 'accepted' ? Colors.green : Colors.orange).withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Text(status.toUpperCase(), style: TextStyle(color: status == 'accepted' ? Colors.green : Colors.orange, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCustomersContent() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('requests')
          .where('donorId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (!snapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        final docs = snapshot.data!.docs;

        // Get unique customers
        Map<String, String> customers = {}; // ID -> Name
        for (var doc in docs) {
          final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
          final rid = data['receiverId'];
          final rname = data['receiverName'] ?? "Receiver";
          if (rid != null) {
            customers[rid] = rname;
          }
        }

        if (customers.isEmpty) return const Center(child: Text("No customers yet."));

        return Column(
          children: customers.keys.map((rid) {
            return ListTile(
              leading: CircleAvatar(
                backgroundColor: Colors.purple.shade50,
                child: Text(customers[rid]![0].toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.purple)),
              ),
              title: Text(customers[rid]!),
              subtitle: const Text("Receiver"),
              trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
            );
          }).toList(),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 60, 20, 30),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [Color(0xFF2E7D32), Color(0xFF66BB6A)],
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(bottomLeft: Radius.circular(40), bottomRight: Radius.circular(40)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text("Hello, $userName 👋", style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 16)),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                const Text("Ready to share\nfood today?", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 28, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, height: 1.2)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          _buildNotificationBell(),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          CircleAvatar(
            radius: 30,
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            child: CircleAvatar(
              radius: 27,
              backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade100,
              child: Text(userName.isNotEmpty ? userName[0].toUpperCase() : '?', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.green)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNotificationBell() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('notifications')
          .where('recipientId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .where('isRead', isEqualTo: false)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        bool hasUnread = snapshot.hasData && snapshot.data!.docs.isNotEmpty;
        return Stack(
          children: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined, color: Colors.white, size: 28),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => NotificationsPage(userId: widget.userId))),
            ),
            if (hasUnread)
              Positioned(right: 8, top: 8, child: Container(width: 12, height: 12, decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red, shape: BoxShape.circle))),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeroActionCard() {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => AddFoodPostPage(userId: widget.userId))).then((_) => loadDashboardData()),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF388E3C), Color(0xFF81C784)]),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.add_business_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 36),
            const SizedBox(width: 20),
            const Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("Donate Food", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  Text("Post your leftover food and help someone in need.", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 14)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, color: Colors.white, size: 18),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatsRow() {
    return Row(
      children: [
        _buildStreamStatCard("Active Posts", "posts", "userID", "status", ["active", "accepted", "claimed", "assigned"], Colors.orange, Icons.inventory_2_rounded),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        _buildStreamStatCard("Earnings", "posts", "userID", "status", ["completed", "delivered"], Colors.blue, Icons.payments_rounded),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        _statCard("Impact", familiesHelped, Colors.green, Icons.volunteer_activism),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStreamStatCard(String title, String collection, String userField, String statusField, List<String> statusValues, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection(collection)
          .where(userField, isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        int count = 0;
        if (snapshot.hasData) {
          count = snapshot.data!.docs.where((doc) => statusValues.contains((doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)[statusField])).length;
        }
        return _statCard(title, count, color, icon);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _statCard(String title, int count, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(count.toString(), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: Colors.grey[600])),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRecentActivityList() {
    if (isLoading) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    if (recentActivity.isEmpty) return const Center(child: Text("No recent activity."));

    return Column(
      children: recentActivity.map((doc) {
        final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final status = data["status"] ?? "active";
        final isDone = status == "completed" || status == "delivered";
        
        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
          child: ListTile(
            leading: CircleAvatar(
              backgroundColor: isDone ? Colors.blue.shade50 : Colors.green.shade50,
              child: Icon(
                isDone ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.restaurant,
                color: isDone ? Colors.blue : Colors.green,
              ),
            ),
            title: Text(data["title"] ?? "No title", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            subtitle: Text(isDone ? "Completed Delivery" : (data["description"] ?? "No description"), maxLines: 1),
            trailing: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isDone ? Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.orange.withOpacity(0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              ),
              child: Text(
                status.toUpperCase(),
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: isDone ? Colors.blue : Colors.orange,
                  fontSize: 10,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _modernBottomNav() {
    return BottomNavigationBar(
      currentIndex: selectedIndex,
      selectedItemColor: Colors.green,
      unselectedItemColor: Colors.grey,
      onTap: (index) {
        setState(() => selectedIndex = index);
        if (index == 0) loadDashboardData();
      },
      items: const [
        BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard), label: "Dashboard"),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: Icon(Icons.list_alt), label: "My Posts"),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: Icon(Icons.mail), label: "Requests"),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: Icon(Icons.person), label: "Profile"),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _AnalyticsDetailSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final Color color;
  final Widget child;

  const _AnalyticsDetailSheet({
    required this.title,
    required this.color,
    required this.child,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.8,
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0xffF8FAFC),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Container(
            width: 40,
            height: 4,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.grey.shade300, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color.withOpacity(0.1), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                  child: Icon(Icons.analytics, color: color, size: 24),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const Spacer(),
                IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.close), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          Expanded(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 40),
              child: child,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'login_screen.dart';

class DonorProfilePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const DonorProfilePage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<DonorProfilePage> createState() => _DonorProfilePageState();
}

class _DonorProfilePageState extends State<DonorProfilePage> {
  bool isLoading = true;

  String userName = "User";
  String email = "";
  String role = "Donor";

  bool isEditing = false;
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    loadProfileData();
  }

  Future<void> loadProfileData() async {
    final doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection("users")
        .doc(widget.userId)
        .get();

    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;

    setState(() {
      userName = data["name"] ?? "User";
      email = data["email"] ?? "";
      role = data["role"] ?? "Donor";
      _nameController.text = userName;
      isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _updateName() async {
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection("users")
        .doc(widget.userId)
        .update({"name": _nameController.text.trim()});

    setState(() {
      userName = _nameController.text.trim();
      isEditing = false;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _logout() async {
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: const Text("Logout", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: const Text("Are you sure you want to log out?"),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text("Cancel", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: Colors.red,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text("Logout", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirm == true) {
      await FirebaseAuth.instance.signOut();
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginScreen()),
          (route) => false,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF5F7F6),
      body: isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green))
          : SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            _header(),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _stats(),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _infoCard(),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _logoutButton(),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ================= HEADER =================
  Widget _header() {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 50, bottom: 30),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [Colors.green, Color(0xff66BB6A)],
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(35),
          bottomRight: Radius.circular(35),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const Text(
            "My Profile",
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Colors.white,
              fontSize: 22,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 15),

          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 3),
            ),
            child: const CircleAvatar(
              radius: 45,
              backgroundColor: Colors.white,
              child: Icon(Icons.person, size: 45, color: Colors.green),
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          isEditing
              ? Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 50),
            child: TextField(
              controller: _nameController,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                filled: true,
                fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                border: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          )
              : Text(
            userName,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 22,
              color: Colors.white,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 5),

          Text(role, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),

          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.white),
                onPressed: () {
                  setState(() {
                    isEditing = !isEditing;
                    _nameController.text = userName;
                  });
                },
              ),
              if (isEditing)
                ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: _updateName,
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                    foregroundColor: Colors.green,
                  ),
                  child: const Text("Save"),
                )
            ],
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ================= STATS =================
  Widget _stats() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          _liveStatBox("Total", "posts", "userID", null, Colors.purple, Icons.fastfood),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          _liveStatBox("Active", "posts", "userID", "active", Colors.orange, Icons.inventory_2),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          _liveStatBox("Done", "posts", "userID", "completed", Colors.green, Icons.check_circle),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _liveStatBox(String label, String collection, String field, String? statusFilter, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return Expanded(
      child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection(collection)
            .where(field, isEqualTo: widget.userId)
            .snapshots(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          int count = 0;
          if (snap.hasData) {
            final docs = snap.data!.docs;
            if (statusFilter != null) {
              count = docs.where((d) => (d.data() as Map)['status'] == statusFilter).length;
            } else {
              count = docs.length;
            }
          }
          return Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05), blurRadius: 10)],
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(icon, color: color, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(count.toString(), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12)),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }




  // ================= INFO =================
  Widget _infoCard() {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          _row(Icons.email, "Email", email),
          const Divider(),
          _row(Icons.person, "Name", userName),
          const Divider(),
          _row(Icons.badge, "Role", role),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _row(IconData icon, String title, String value) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: Colors.green),
      title: Text(title),
      subtitle: Text(value),
    );
  }

  // ================= LOGOUT =================
  Widget _logoutButton() {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
            blurRadius: 20,
          )
        ],
      ),
      child: _buildActionTile(
        Icons.logout,
        "Logout",
        "Sign out of your account",
        _logout,
        isDestructive: true,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionTile(IconData icon, String title, String subtitle, VoidCallback onTap, {bool isDestructive = false}) {
    return ListTile(
      onTap: onTap,
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
      leading: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red.shade50 : Colors.green.shade50,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        ),
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red : Colors.green,
          size: 24,
        ),
      ),
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red : Colors.black87,
        ),
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        subtitle,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12,
          color: Colors.grey.shade500,
        ),
      ),
      trailing: Icon(
        Icons.chevron_right,
        color: Colors.grey.shade400,
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

class EventsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String ngoId;
  const EventsPage({super.key, required this.ngoId});

  @override
  State<EventsPage> createState() => _EventsPageState();
}

class _EventsPageState extends State<EventsPage> {
  final drivesRef = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("drives");

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF8FAF9),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // HEADER
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        width: 40,
                        height: 40,
                        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                          color: Color(0xFF2E7D32),
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        ),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.eco, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      const Text(
                        "Vitality Food",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          color: Color(0xFF2E7D32),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  Container(
                    width: 40,
                    height: 40,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(
                          color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
                          blurRadius: 8,
                          offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.notifications_none, color: Color(0xFF2E7D32), size: 20),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // TITLE
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    "Active Events",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 28,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Color(0xFF1B1B1B),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    "Manage and track your community food drives.",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                      height: 1.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 20),

            // DEBUG INFO
            Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.amber.shade100,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.info_outline, color: Colors.amber.shade800),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      "NGO ID: ${widget.ngoId}",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: Colors.amber.shade900,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 12),

            // EVENTS LIST - GET ALL DOCUMENTS, FILTER LOCALLY
            Expanded(
              child: StreamBuilder(
                stream: drivesRef.snapshots(), // Get ALL drives, no filtering
                builder: (context, snap) {
                  // Handle errors
                  if (snap.hasError) {
                    return Center(
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48, color: Colors.red),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          const Text(
                            "Error loading events",
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 40),
                            child: Text(
                              "${snap.error}",
                              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 12),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          ElevatedButton(
                            onPressed: () => setState(() {}),
                            child: const Text("Retry"),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  }

                  // Loading
                  if (!snap.hasData) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }

                  final data = snap.data!.docs;

                  // DEBUG: Show how many total documents
                  debugPrint("Total drives in collection: ${data.length}");
                  for (var doc in data) {
                    final d = doc.data() as Map;
                    debugPrint("Drive: ${d["name"]}, ngoId: ${d["ngoId"]}, status: ${d["status"]}");
                  }

                  if (data.isEmpty) {
                    return _buildEmptyState();
                  }

                  // Show ALL events (remove filtering for now to test)
                  // Later you can add back: .where((doc) => doc["ngoId"] == widget.ngoId)
                  return ListView.builder(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
                    itemCount: data.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final drive = data[index].data() as Map;
                      final driveId = data[index].id;
                      final progress = (drive["progress"] ?? 0.0).toDouble();
                      final goal = drive["mealGoal"] ?? drive["goal"] ?? 500;
                      final current = (progress * goal).toInt();
                      final percent = (progress * 100).toInt();

                      return _buildEventCard(
                        driveId: driveId,
                        name: drive["name"] ?? "Unnamed Event",
                        location: drive["location"] ?? "No location",
                        date: drive["date"] != null
                            ? DateFormat('MMM dd, yyyy').format((drive["date"] as Timestamp).toDate())
                            : "Date TBD",
                        time: drive["startTime"] ?? "--:--",
                        volunteerGoal: drive["volunteerGoal"] ?? 0,
                        assignedVolunteers: (drive["assignedVolunteers"] as List?)?.length ?? 0,
                        mealGoal: goal,
                        currentMeals: current,
                        percent: percent,
                        description: drive["description"] ?? "",
                      );
                    },
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () => _navigateToCreateEvent(context),
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF2E7D32),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
        label: const Text(
          "New Event",
          style: TextStyle(
            color: Colors.white,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 80,
            height: 80,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFFE8F5E9),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.event_outlined,
              color: Color(0xFF2E7D32),
              size: 40,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          const Text(
            "No Events Found",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              color: Color(0xFF1B1B1B),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            "Your drives collection is empty.\nCreate an event or check Firestore.",
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Colors.grey.shade600,
              fontSize: 14,
            ),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => _navigateToCreateEvent(context),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            label: const Text("Create Event"),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF2E7D32),
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,

  }

      child: Column(

            ),

              children: [
                Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: const Color(0xFF2E7D32).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        ),
                        child: const Icon(
                          Icons.image_outlined,
                          color: Color(0xFF2E7D32),
                          size: 32,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text(
                        "Add Banner",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: const Color(0xFF2E7D32).withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                          fontSize: 12,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                // Status Badge
                Positioned(
                  top: 12,
                  right: 12,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: const Color(0xFF2E7D32),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                    ),
                    child: const Text(
                      "Active",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      ),

              ],
            ),

          // Content Section
          Padding(

            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                // Title

                  name,

                  ),

                const SizedBox(height: 12),
