            _buildSectionTitle('Performance Overview'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildPerformanceCards(),
            const SizedBox(height: 32),
            _buildSectionTitle('\u{1F916} AI Food Safety Analytics'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildAiAnalytics(),
            const SizedBox(height: 32),
            _buildSectionTitle('\u{1F4CA} AI Predictive Insights'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildPredictiveInsights(),
            const SizedBox(height: 32),
            _buildSectionTitle('Volunteer Performance'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildVolunteerPerformance(),
            const SizedBox(height: 32),
            _buildSectionTitle('Recent Accomplishments'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _buildRecentAccomplishments(),
            const SizedBox(height: 40),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── HEADER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildHeader() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 60, 24, 40),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [Color(0xFF1A237E), Color(0xFF3F51B5)],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(40),
          bottomRight: Radius.circular(40),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('Impact Analytics',
                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 16)),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text('Organization Reports',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 26,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                ],
              ),
              // Export button
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                onPressed: _showExportDialog,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.download, size: 16),
                label: const Text('Export'),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                  foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF1A237E),
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
                  elevation: 0,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          // Glassmorphism stats card – scoped to this NGO
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
            child: BackdropFilter(
              filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                  border:
                      Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                  children: [
                    // Meals Delivered = tasks by this NGO with status=delivered
                    StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('tasks')
                          .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                          .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
                          .snapshots(),
                      builder: (context, snap) => _heroStat(
                          snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length.toString() : '0',
                          'Meals\nDelivered',
                          Icons.restaurant),
                    ),
                    Container(width: 1, height: 40, color: Colors.white24),
                    // Active volunteers who have ever worked with this NGO
                    StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('tasks')
                          .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                          .snapshots(),
                      builder: (context, snap) {
                        int uniqueVols = 0;
                        if (snap.hasData) {
                          final ids = snap.data!.docs
                              .map((d) =>
                                  (d.data() as Map)['assignedTo'] as String? ??
                                  '')
                              .where((id) => id.isNotEmpty)
                              .toSet();
                          uniqueVols = ids.length;
                        }
                        return _heroStat(uniqueVols.toString(),
                            'Volunteers\nEngaged', Icons.people);
                      },
                    ),
                    Container(width: 1, height: 40, color: Colors.white24),
                    // Receiver requests accepted by this NGO
                    StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('requests')
                          .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                          .where('status', isEqualTo: 'accepted')
                          .snapshots(),
                      builder: (context, snap) => _heroStat(
                          snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length.toString() : '0',
                          'Families\nHelped',
                          Icons.volunteer_activism),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── AI ANALYTICS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildAiAnalytics() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('posts')
            .where('aiScanEnabled', isEqualTo: true)
            .snapshots(),
        builder: (context, allSnap) {
          if (!allSnap.hasData) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.indigo));
          }

          final docs = allSnap.data!.docs;
          int totalScans = docs.length;
          int unsafeBlocked = 0;
          int highRisk = 0;
          int totalScore = 0;
          int scoredCount = 0;
          final Map<String, int> categoryCount = {};

          for (final doc in docs) {
            final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
            final freshness = d['aiFreshnessStatus'] as String? ?? '';
            final modPassed = d['aiModerationPassed'] as bool? ?? true;
            final score = (d['aiFreshnessScore'] as num?)?.toInt();
            final category = d['aiFoodCategory'] as String? ?? 'other';

            if (freshness == 'unsafe' || !modPassed) unsafeBlocked++;
            if (freshness == 'expiringSoon' || freshness == 'unsafe') highRisk++;
            if (score != null) {
              totalScore += score;
              scoredCount++;
            }
            categoryCount[category] = (categoryCount[category] ?? 0) + 1;
          }

          final avgScore = scoredCount > 0 ? (totalScore / scoredCount).round() : 0;
          final topCategory = categoryCount.isEmpty
              ? 'N/A'
              : (categoryCount.entries.toList()
                    ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value)))
                  .first
                  .key;
          final wasteSaved = unsafeBlocked * 3;

          return Column(
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('Total AI Scans', '$totalScans', Icons.document_scanner_outlined, Colors.indigo)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('Unsafe Blocked', '$unsafeBlocked', Icons.block, Colors.red.shade700)),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('High-Risk Handled', '$highRisk', Icons.warning_amber, Colors.orange.shade700)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('Avg Freshness Score', '$avgScore/100', Icons.star_outline, Colors.green.shade700)),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('Top Food Category', _categoryLabel(topCategory), Icons.category_outlined, Colors.teal.shade700)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(child: _aiMetricCard('Waste Prevented', '~${wasteSaved}kg', Icons.eco_outlined, Colors.green.shade700)),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.indigo.shade50,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.indigo.shade100),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, color: Colors.indigo.shade400, size: 20),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'AI scanning prevents unsafe food distribution and prioritises urgent donations for faster pickup.',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.indigo.shade700, fontSize: 12, height: 1.4),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _aiMetricCard(String label, String value, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04), blurRadius: 10),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            value,
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.grey.shade600, height: 1.3),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _categoryLabel(String cat) {
    const labels = {
      'fruits': 'Fruits',
      'vegetables': 'Vegetables',
      'cookedMeals': 'Meals',
      'bakery': 'Bakery',
      'dairy': 'Dairy',
      'meat': 'Meat',
      'beverages': 'Beverages',
      'other': 'Other',
    };
    return labels[cat] ?? cat;
  }

  // ─── PERFORMANCE CARDS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildPerformanceCards() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Claimed Donations card
          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection('posts')
                .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                .snapshots(),
            builder: (context, snap) {
              final total = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
              final claimed = snap.hasData
                  ? snap.data!.docs
                      .where((d) =>
                          (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'claimed')
                      .length
                  : 0;
              final completed = snap.hasData
                  ? snap.data!.docs
                      .where((d) =>
                          (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'completed')
                      .length
                  : 0;
              return _reportCard(
                title: 'Donations Managed',
                subtitle:
                    '$total total  •  $claimed active  •  $completed completed',
                color: Colors.green,
                icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                onTap: () => _showDetailSheet(
                    context, 'Claimed Donations', snap.data?.docs ?? []),
              );
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          // Pending requests card
          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection('requests')
                .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                .snapshots(),
            builder: (context, snap) {
              final total = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
              final pending = snap.hasData
                  ? snap.data!.docs
                      .where((d) =>
                          (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'pending')
                      .length
                  : 0;
              return _reportCard(
                title: 'Receiver Requests',
                subtitle: '$total total  •  $pending still pending',
                color: Colors.orange,
                icon: Icons.inbox_outlined,
                onTap: () => _showDetailSheet(
                    context, 'All Requests', snap.data?.docs ?? []),
              );
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          // Active drives card
          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection('drives')
                .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
                .snapshots(),
            builder: (context, snap) {
              final total = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
              return _reportCard(
                title: 'Food Drives Created',
                subtitle: '$total drives organised by your NGO',
                color: Colors.blue,
                icon: Icons.campaign_outlined,
                onTap: () => _showDetailSheet(
                    context, 'Food Drives', snap.data?.docs ?? []),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── VOLUNTEER PERFORMANCE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildVolunteerPerformance() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('tasks')
            .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
            .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
            .snapshots(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (!snap.hasData || snap.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return _emptyCard(
                'No completed deliveries yet.',
                'Once volunteers complete tasks for your NGO, their stats will appear here.');
          }

          // Aggregate deliveries per volunteer
          final Map<String, int> volCount = {};
          for (final doc in snap.data!.docs) {
            final id =
                (doc.data() as Map)['assignedTo'] as String? ?? '';
            if (id.isNotEmpty) volCount[id] = (volCount[id] ?? 0) + 1;
          }

          // Sort descending
          final sorted = volCount.entries.toList()
            ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value));

          return Column(
            children: sorted.take(5).map((entry) {
              return FutureBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
                future: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                    .collection('users')
                    .doc(entry.key)
                    .get(),
                builder: (context, uSnap) {
                  final name = uSnap.hasData && uSnap.data!.exists
                      ? (uSnap.data!.data() as Map)['name'] ?? 'Volunteer'
                      : 'Loading…';
                  return Container(
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                            blurRadius: 10)
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        CircleAvatar(
                          radius: 22,
                          backgroundColor: Colors.indigo.shade50,
                          child: Text(
                            name.isNotEmpty ? name[0].toUpperCase() : '?',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.indigo,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 14),
                        Expanded(
                            child: Text(name,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.green.shade50,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            '${entry.value} deliveries',
                            style: TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.green.shade700,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 12),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  );
                },
              );
            }).toList(),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── ACCOMPLISHMENTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildRecentAccomplishments() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('tasks')
            .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: _ngoId)
            .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
            .orderBy('createdAt', descending: true)
            .limit(5)
            .snapshots(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (!snap.hasData || snap.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return _emptyCard(
                'No completed deliveries yet.',
                'When a volunteer marks a delivery as done it will appear here.');
          }
          return Column(
            children: snap.data!.docs.map((doc) {
              final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final title = d['title'] ?? 'Delivery';
              final ts = (d['createdAt'] as Timestamp?)?.toDate();
              final date =
                  ts != null ? DateFormat('MMM dd, yyyy').format(ts) : '';
              return Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  border:
                      Border.all(color: Colors.green.shade100),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.green.shade50,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.check_circle,
                          color: Colors.green, size: 20),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(title,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 14)),
                          if (date.isNotEmpty)
                            Text(date,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                    color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.green.shade50,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: const Text('Delivered',
                          style: TextStyle(
                              color: Colors.green,
                              fontSize: 11,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            }).toList(),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _heroStat(String value, String label, IconData icon) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 24),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text(value,
            style: const TextStyle(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(label,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style:
                const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11)),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _reportCard({
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
    required Color color,
    required IconData icon,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
  }) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
                color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                blurRadius: 20)
          ],
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
              child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 20),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(title,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(subtitle,
                      style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 13)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Colors.grey.shade400),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionTitle(String title) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: Text(title,
          style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.black87)),
    );
  }

  Widget _emptyCard(String title, String subtitle) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 0),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade100),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.bar_chart, size: 48, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(title,
              style: const TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.black87)),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(subtitle,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 13)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── DETAIL BOTTOM SHEET ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _showDetailSheet(
      BuildContext context, String title, List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> docs) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30))),
      builder: (_) => DraggableScrollableSheet(
        initialChildSize: 0.6,
        maxChildSize: 0.9,
        minChildSize: 0.4,
        expand: false,
        builder: (_, controller) => Column(
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Container(
                width: 40,
                height: 4,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4))),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: docs.isEmpty
                  ? const Center(child: Text('No records found.'))
                  : ListView.builder(
                      controller: controller,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
                      itemCount: docs.length,
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                        final d = docs[i].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                        final label = d['title'] ??
                            d['name'] ??
                            d['itemTitle'] ??
                            'Record #${i + 1}';
                        final status = d['status'] ?? '';
                        return ListTile(
                          leading: const Icon(Icons.circle,
                              size: 10, color: Colors.green),
                          title: Text(label),
                          trailing: status.isNotEmpty
                              ? Chip(
                                  label: Text(status,
                                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                                  backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade50,
                                  side: BorderSide.none,
                                )
                              : null,
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── EXPORT DIALOG ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  void _showExportDialog() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: const Text('Export Report',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: const Text(
            'A summary report of your NGO activity will be prepared.\n\n'
            'Export formats: PDF and CSV will be available in a future release. '
            'For now your data is safely stored in Firestore.'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              child: const Text('Close', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey))),
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                    content: Text('Report data ready. PDF export coming soon.'),
                    backgroundColor: Colors.indigo),
              );
            },
            icon: const Icon(Icons.download, size: 16),
            label: const Text('Confirm'),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF1A237E),
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── LIVE AI MONITORING ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildLiveMonitoring() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: FutureBuilder<MonitoringSnapshot>(
        future: AiMonitoringService.getMonitoringSnapshot(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (!snap.hasData) {
            return const Center(
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.indigo),
              ),
            );
          }
          final m = snap.data!;
          return Column(
            children: [
              // Pulsing live indicator
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: const LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Color(0xFF1A237E), Color(0xFF3949AB)],
                  ),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    const Row(
                      children: [
                        _PulsingDot(),
                        SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text('Live Monitoring Active',
                            style: TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 13)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    Text(
                      'Updated ${DateFormat('HH:mm').format(m.computedAt)}',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              // Monitoring metric grid
              GridView.count(
                crossAxisCount: 2,
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                crossAxisSpacing: 12,
                mainAxisSpacing: 12,
                childAspectRatio: 1.5,
                children: [
                  _monitorCard(
                    '${m.activeUrgentDonations}',
                    'Active Urgent\nDonations',
                    Icons.local_fire_department,
                    m.activeUrgentDonations > 0
                        ? Colors.red
                        : Colors.green,
                  ),
                  _monitorCard(
                    '${m.deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk}',
                    'Spoilage Risk\nDeliveries',
                    Icons.thermostat,
                    m.deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk > 0
                        ? Colors.orange
                        : Colors.green,
                  ),
                  _monitorCard(
                    '${m.unsafeUploadsBlocked}',
                    'Unsafe Uploads\nBlocked',
                    Icons.security,
                    Colors.indigo,
                  ),
                  _monitorCard(
                    m.avgDeliveryMinutes > 0
                        ? '${m.avgDeliveryMinutes.round()}min'
                        : 'N/A',
                    'Avg Delivery\nTime',
                    Icons.timer_outlined,
                    Colors.blue,
                  ),
                  _monitorCard(
                    '~${m.foodWastePreventedKg.round()}kg',
                    'Food Waste\nPrevented',
                    Icons.eco,
                    Colors.green,
                  ),
                  _monitorCard(
                    '${m.aiInterventionSuccessRate.round()}%',
                    'AI Intervention\nSuccess Rate',
                    Icons.auto_awesome,
                    m.aiInterventionSuccessRate >= 70
                        ? Colors.green
                        : Colors.orange,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _monitorCard(
      String value, String label, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
              color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              blurRadius: 12,
              offset: const Offset(0, 4))
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(value,
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 22,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: color)),
          Text(label,
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 10,
                  color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                  height: 1.3)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─── PREDICTIVE INSIGHTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Widget _buildPredictiveInsights() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      child: FutureBuilder<List<PredictiveInsight>>(
        future: AiMonitoringService.getPredictiveInsights(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (!snap.hasData) {
            return const Center(
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.teal),
              ),
            );
          }
          final insights = snap.data!;
          return Column(
            children: insights
                .map((insight) => _insightCard(insight))
                .toList(),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _insightCard(PredictiveInsight insight) {
    final trendColor = insight.trend == '↑'
        ? Colors.green
        : insight.trend == '↓'
            ? Colors.red
            : Colors.orange;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
              blurRadius: 10,
              offset: const Offset(0, 4))
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: trendColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            ),
            child: Text(
              insight.trend,
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: trendColor),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text(insight.label,
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                    Text(insight.value,
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: trendColor)),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  insight.insight,
                  style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12,
                      color: Colors.grey.shade700,
                      height: 1.4),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ─── Pulsing dot widget ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class _PulsingDot extends StatefulWidget {
  const _PulsingDot();

  @override
  State<_PulsingDot> createState() => _PulsingDotState();
}

class _PulsingDotState extends State<_PulsingDot>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _ctrl;
  late Animation<double> _anim;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _ctrl = AnimationController(
        vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900))
      ..repeat(reverse: true);
    _anim = Tween(begin: 0.4, end: 1.0).animate(_ctrl);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ctrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _anim,
      builder: (_, __) => Container(
        width: 8,
        height: 8,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          color: Colors.greenAccent.withValues(alpha: _anim.value),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: lib\ngo_requests_page.dart
================================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'task_service.dart';
import 'notification_service.dart';

class NGORequestsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const NGORequestsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<NGORequestsPage> createState() => _NGORequestsPageState();
}

class _NGORequestsPageState extends State<NGORequestsPage> {
  // Cache outer stream — scoped to this NGO's claimed posts
  Stream<QuerySnapshot>? _postsStream;
  // Cache inner requests stream to avoid flash-to-empty
  Stream<QuerySnapshot>? _requestsStream;
  List<String> _lastClaimedPostIds = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final currentNgoId = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';
    _postsStream = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('posts')
        .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: currentNgoId)
        .snapshots();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF7F9FC),
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Receiver Requests',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: _buildRequestList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRequestList() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: _postsStream,
      builder: (context, postsSnap) {
        if (!postsSnap.hasData) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));
        }

        final claimedPostIds = postsSnap.data!.docs.map((d) => d.id).toList();

        if (claimedPostIds.isEmpty) {
          return _emptyState(
            'No claimed donations yet',
            'Claim a donation first so receivers can send you requests.',
          );
        }

        // ── PHASE 3 FIX: chunk whereIn into batches of 10 ──────────────
        // Firestore whereIn limit is 30; we use 10 for safety margin.
        final newKey = claimedPostIds.join(',');
        final oldKey = _lastClaimedPostIds.join(',');

        if (newKey != oldKey) {
          _lastClaimedPostIds = List.from(claimedPostIds);
          // Build a merged real-time stream by fetching all at once.
          // Because we can't easily merge Firestore streams client-side,
          // we query with a Firestore stream on the first chunk and reload
          // when the outer stream changes. For >10 posts we fall back to
          // one-time batched fetch inside the StreamBuilder pattern below.
          if (claimedPostIds.length <= 10) {
            _requestsStream = FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection('requests')
                .where('itemId', whereIn: claimedPostIds)
                .snapshots();
          } else {
            // For >10, query by ngoId directly on the requests collection.
            // Requests now include ngoId when receiver sends request to a
            // claimed post (see ReceiverHomePage._sendRequest fix).
            final currentNgoId = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';
            _requestsStream = FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection('requests')
                .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: currentNgoId)
                .snapshots();
          }
        }

        return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
          stream: _requestsStream,
          builder: (context, reqSnap) {
            if (!reqSnap.hasData) {
              return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));
            }

            final allRequests = reqSnap.data!.docs;
            final pending =
                allRequests.where((d) => (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'pending').toList();
            final accepted =
                allRequests.where((d) => (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'accepted').toList();
            final rejected =
                allRequests.where((d) => (d.data() as Map)['status'] == 'rejected').toList();

            if (allRequests.isEmpty) {
              return _emptyState(
                'No requests yet',
                'Once receivers request your claimed donations, they will appear here.',
              );
            }

            return DefaultTabController(
              length: 3,
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    child: TabBar(
                      labelColor: Colors.green,
                      unselectedLabelColor: Colors.grey,
                      indicatorColor: Colors.green,
                      tabs: [
                        Tab(text: 'Pending (${pending.length})'),
                        Tab(text: 'Accepted (${accepted.length})'),
                        Tab(text: 'Rejected (${rejected.length})'),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Expanded(
                    child: TabBarView(
                      children: [
                        _requestList(pending, showActions: true),
                        _requestList(accepted, showActions: false),
                        _requestList(rejected, showActions: false),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _requestList(List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> requests, {required bool showActions}) {
    if (requests.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(
          child: Text('No requests in this category.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)));
    }

    return ListView.builder(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      itemCount: requests.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final req = requests[index].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final receiverName = req['receiverName'] ?? 'Receiver';
        final itemTitle = req['itemTitle'] ?? req['requestedItem'] ?? 'Food Item';
        final createdAt = (req['timestamp'] as Timestamp?)?.toDate() ?? DateTime.now();
        final status = req['status'] ?? 'pending';

        Color statusColor = Colors.orange;
        if (status == 'accepted') statusColor = Colors.green;
        if (status == 'rejected') statusColor = Colors.red;

        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04), blurRadius: 20)
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  CircleAvatar(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade50,
                    child: const Icon(Icons.person_outline, color: Colors.green),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(receiverName,
                            style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
                        Text(DateFormat('MMM dd • hh:mm a').format(createdAt),
                            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 12)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                    child: Text(status.toUpperCase(),
                        style: TextStyle(
                            color: statusColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text('Requested: $itemTitle',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              if (showActions) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton(
                        onPressed: () async {
                          await requests[index].reference.update({'status': 'rejected'});

                          // Notify receiver of rejection
                          final receiverId = req['receiverId'] ?? '';
                          if (receiverId.isNotEmpty) {
                            await NotificationService.sendNotification(
                              recipientId: receiverId,
                              title: 'Request Rejected',
                              message: 'Your request for "$itemTitle" was not accepted.',
                              type: 'rejection',
                              relatedId: requests[index].id,
                              relatedType: 'request',
                            );
                          }

                          if (context.mounted) {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                  content: Text('Request rejected.'),
                                  backgroundColor: Colors.red),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.red),
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        ),
                        child: const Text('Reject',
                            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: () async {
                          final currentNgoId =
                              FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';
                          final itemId = req['itemId'] ?? '';
                          final receiverId = req['receiverId'] ?? '';
                          final donorId = req['donorId'] ?? '';
                          final requestId = requests[index].id;

                          // 1. Accept the request
                          await requests[index].reference.update({'status': 'accepted'});

                          // 2. Fetch post details for task fields
                          String pickup = 'Donor Location';
                          if (itemId.isNotEmpty) {
                            try {
                              final postDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
                                  .collection('posts')
                                  .doc(itemId)
                                  .get();
                              if (postDoc.exists) {
                                pickup = (postDoc.data()?['location'] as String?) ??
                                    'Donor Location';
                              }
                            } catch (_) {}
                          }

                          // 3. Auto-create delivery task
                          await TaskService.createDeliveryTask(
                            ngoId: currentNgoId,
                            postId: itemId,
                            requestId: requestId,
                            donorId: donorId,
                            receiverId: receiverId,
                            title: itemTitle,
                            pickup: pickup,
                            dropoff: 'Receiver: $receiverName',
                            receiverName: receiverName,
                          );

                          // 4. Notify receiver
                          if (receiverId.isNotEmpty) {
                            await NotificationService.sendNotification(
                              recipientId: receiverId,
                              title: 'Request Accepted! 🎉',
                              message:
                                  'Your request for "$itemTitle" was accepted. A volunteer will deliver it soon.',
                              type: 'approval',
                              relatedId: requestId,
                              relatedType: 'request',
                            );
                          }

                          if (context.mounted) {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content: Text('Approved! Delivery task created for volunteers.'),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                              ),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                          elevation: 0,
                        ),
                        child: const Text('Approve',
                            style:
                                TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _emptyState(String title, String subtitle) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.mark_email_read_outlined, size: 80, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.black87, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(subtitle,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 14, height: 1.5)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: lib\ngo_volunteers_page.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'notification_service.dart';
import 'services/ai_recommendation_service.dart';

class NGOVolunteersPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const NGOVolunteersPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<NGOVolunteersPage> createState() => _NGOVolunteersPageState();
}

class _NGOVolunteersPageState extends State<NGOVolunteersPage> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF7F9FC),
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Manage Volunteers',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
            .collection('users')
            .where('role', isEqualTo: 'Volunteer')
            .snapshots(),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.hasError) {
            return Center(
                child: Text('Error: ${snapshot.error}',
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red)));
          }
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));
          }

          if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.green.shade50, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                      child: Icon(Icons.people_outline,
                          size: 64, color: Colors.green.shade300),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    const Text('No Volunteers Yet',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 22,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            color: Colors.black87)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Text(
                      'When users sign up as volunteers, they will appear here.',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style:
                          TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 16, height: 1.5),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            );
          }

          final volunteers = snapshot.data!.docs;

          return ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            itemCount: volunteers.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final doc = volunteers[index];
              final v = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final name = v['name'] as String? ?? 'Volunteer';
              final email = v['email'] as String? ?? '';
              final status = v['status'] as String? ?? 'Available';

              final statusColor = status == 'Available'
                  ? Colors.green
                  : status == 'Busy'
                      ? Colors.orange
                      : Colors.grey;

              return Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                        blurRadius: 10)
                  ],
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    CircleAvatar(
                      radius: 28,
                      backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade50,
                      child: Text(
                        name[0].toUpperCase(),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.green,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontSize: 20),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 16),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(name,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text(email,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 13)),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                                .collection('tasks')
                                .where('assignedTo', isEqualTo: doc.id)
                                .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
                                .snapshots(),
                            builder: (context, snap) {
                              final count =
                                  snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
                              return Text(
                                'Deliveries completed: $count',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.green.shade700,
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Column(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                          child: Text(status,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                  color: statusColor,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 12)),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        TextButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () =>
                              _showAssignTaskDialog(doc.id, name),
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.assignment_ind, size: 16),
                          label: const Text('Assign'),
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                            minimumSize: const Size(0, 0),
                            tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // AI-enhanced task assignment dialog
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  void _showAssignTaskDialog(String volunteerId, String volunteerName) {
    final currentNgoId = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '';

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(30))),
      builder: (_) => DraggableScrollableSheet(
        initialChildSize: 0.75,
        minChildSize: 0.5,
        maxChildSize: 0.95,
        expand: false,
        builder: (_, controller) => StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
              .collection('tasks')
              .where('ngoId', isEqualTo: currentNgoId)
              .where('status', isEqualTo: 'pending')
              .snapshots(),
          builder: (context, snapshot) {
            if (!snapshot.hasData) {
              return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
            }
            final tasks = snapshot.data!.docs;

            // Sort tasks by AI urgency for smarter assignment
            tasks.sort((a, b) {
              final ad = a.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final bd = b.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              int urgencyRank(String? u) =>
                  u == 'critical' ? 0 : u == 'urgent' ? 1 : 2;
              return urgencyRank(ad['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String?)
                  .compareTo(urgencyRank(bd['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String?));
            });

            return ListView(
              controller: controller,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                // Handle indicator
                Center(
                  child: Container(
                    width: 40,
                    height: 4,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                Text(
                  'Assign Task to $volunteerName',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  'AI-sorted by urgency. Select a pending task below.',
                  style:
                      TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 13),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),

                if (tasks.isEmpty)
                  const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 40),
                    child: Center(
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.assignment_turned_in,
                              size: 60, color: Colors.grey),
                          SizedBox(height: 16),
                          Text('No pending deliveries available',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.grey,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )
                else
                  ...tasks.map((taskDoc) {
                    final t = taskDoc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                    final aiUrgency =
                        t['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
                    final spoilageRisk =
                        t['spoilageRiskDuringDelivery'] as String? ?? 'low';
                    final predictedMins =
                        (t['predictedDeliveryMinutes'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 30;
                    final isUrgent =
                        aiUrgency == 'critical' || aiUrgency == 'urgent';

                    return Container(
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        border: Border.all(
                          color: isUrgent
                              ? const Color(0xFFE67E22).withValues(alpha: 0.5)
                              : Colors.grey.shade200,
                          width: isUrgent ? 2 : 1,
                        ),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                        color: isUrgent
                            ? const Color(0xFFFFF8F2)
                            : Colors.white,
                      ),
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Row(
                              children: [
                                Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: isUrgent
                                        ? Colors.orange.shade50
                                        : Colors.grey.shade50,
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                  ),
                                  child: Icon(
                                    Icons.delivery_dining,
                                    color: isUrgent
                                        ? Colors.orange.shade700
                                        : Colors.grey,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Column(
                                    crossAxisAlignment:
                                        CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        t['title'] ?? 'Delivery',
                                        style: const TextStyle(
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                            fontSize: 15),
                                      ),
                                      Text(
                                        '${t['pickup']} → ${t['dropoff']}',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                            fontSize: 11,
                                            color: Colors.grey.shade600),
                                        maxLines: 1,
                                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            // AI metadata row
                            Wrap(
                              spacing: 8,
                              children: [
                                _taskChip('~${predictedMins}min',
                                    Icons.timer, Colors.blue.shade700),
                                if (aiUrgency != 'normal')
                                  _taskChip(
                                    aiUrgency.toUpperCase(),
                                    Icons.warning_amber,
                                    aiUrgency == 'critical'
                                        ? Colors.red
                                        : Colors.orange,
                                  ),
                                if (spoilageRisk == 'high' ||
                                    spoilageRisk == 'critical')
                                  _taskChip(
                                      'Spoilage Risk',
                                      Icons.thermostat,
                                      Colors.red.shade700),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            SizedBox(
                              width: double.infinity,
                              child: ElevatedButton(
                                onPressed: () =>
                                    _assignTask(taskDoc.id, volunteerId, t,
                                        volunteerName),
                                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                  backgroundColor: isUrgent
                                      ? const Color(0xFFE67E22)
                                      : Colors.green,
                                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                      borderRadius:
                                          BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                                  elevation: 0,
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      vertical: 12),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  isUrgent ? 'Assign (Urgent)' : 'Assign',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _taskChip(String label, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 11, color: color),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Text(label,
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: color)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _assignTask(
      String taskId, String volunteerId, Map<String, dynamic> taskData,
      String volunteerName) async {
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('tasks').doc(taskId).update({
      'assignedTo': volunteerId,
      'status': 'assigned',
      'assignedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });

    if (taskData['postId'] != null) {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('posts')
          .doc(taskData['postId'] as String)
          .update({'status': 'assigned', 'volunteerId': volunteerId});
    }

    await NotificationService.sendNotification(
      recipientId: volunteerId,
      title: (taskData['aiUrgencyLevel'] == 'critical' ||
              taskData['aiUrgencyLevel'] == 'urgent')
          ? '⚡ Urgent Task Assigned!'
          : 'New Task Assigned!',
      message:
          'You have been assigned: "${taskData['title'] ?? "a delivery task"}".',
      type: 'task',
      relatedId: taskId,
    );

    // Invalidate volunteer recommendation cache for this NGO
    AiRecommendationService.clearCache();

    if (mounted) {
      Navigator.pop(context);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Task assigned to $volunteerName!'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
        ),
      );
    }
  }
}
