import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

class NotificationsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const NotificationsPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<NotificationsPage> createState() => _NotificationsPageState();
}

class _NotificationsPageState extends State<NotificationsPage> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _markAllAsRead();
  }

  Future<void> _markAllAsRead() async {
    try {
      final snp = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('notifications')
          .where('recipientId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .where('isRead', isEqualTo: false)
          .get();
      
      if (snp.docs.isEmpty) return;

      final batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
      for (var doc in snp.docs) {
        batch.update(doc.reference, {'isRead': true});
      }
      await batch.commit();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error marking as read: $e");
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(
          "Notifications",
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 24),
        ),
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_new, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned.fill(
            child: Container(
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  colors: [Color(0xFF1B5E20), Color(0xFF2E7D32), Color(0xFF43A047)],
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          SafeArea(
            child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
              stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                  .collection('notifications')
                  .where('recipientId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
                  .snapshots(),
              builder: (context, snapshot) {
                if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                  return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white));
                }

                if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                  return _buildModernEmptyState();
                }

                // Manual Sort to avoid index issues or null timestamp disappearance
                final docs = snapshot.data!.docs.toList();
                docs.sort((a, b) {
                  final aTime = (a.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                  final bTime = (b.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
                  if (aTime == null || bTime == null) return 0;
                  return bTime.compareTo(aTime);
                });

                return ListView.builder(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 10, 20, 40),
                  physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                  itemCount: docs.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final doc = docs[index];
                    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                    return _buildPremiumCard(doc.id, data);
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPremiumCard(String id, Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final bool isRead = data['isRead'] ?? false;
    final String type = data['type'] ?? 'system';
    final Timestamp? ts = data['timestamp'] as Timestamp?;
    final String timeStr = ts != null ? DateFormat('h:mm a').format(ts.toDate()) : '';

    IconData icon;
    Color accentColor;
    switch (type) {
      case 'request': icon = Icons.local_mall_rounded; accentColor = Colors.orangeAccent; break;
      case 'approval': icon = Icons.verified_rounded; accentColor = Colors.cyanAccent; break;
      case 'rejection': icon = Icons.error_outline_rounded; accentColor = Colors.redAccent; break;
      default: icon = Icons.notifications_active_rounded; accentColor = Colors.white;
    }

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        child: BackdropFilter(
          filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isRead ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
              border: Border.all(
                color: isRead ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.white.withOpacity(0.3),
                width: 1.5,
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: accentColor.withOpacity(0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(icon, color: accentColor, size: 24),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            data['title'] ?? 'Notification',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            timeStr,
                            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.6), fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        data['message'] ?? '',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85), fontSize: 14, height: 1.4, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildModernEmptyState() {
    return Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(30),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.1), shape: BoxShape.circle),
            child: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, size: 80, color: Colors.white70),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          const Text("Nothing here yet", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Colors.white)),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text("We'll buzz you when you get a new update!", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7), fontSize: 16)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: lib\notification_service.dart
================================================================================

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

/// Centralized notification writer.
/// Writes to the Firestore `notifications` collection.
/// relatedType clarifies what relatedId points to: "post"|"request"|"task"|"drive"
class NotificationService {
  static Future<void> sendNotification({
    required String recipientId,
    required String title,
    required String message,
    required String type,
    String? relatedId,
    String? relatedType,
  }) async {
    if (recipientId.isEmpty) return;
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('notifications').add({
        'recipientId': recipientId,
        'title': title,
        'message': message,
        'type': type,
        'relatedId': relatedId ?? '',
        'relatedType': relatedType ?? '',
        'isRead': false,
        'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      // Notification failure should not crash the app
    }
  }

  /// Notify all users of a given role (batched to avoid whereIn limits)
  static Future<void> notifyAllByRole({
    required String role,
    required String title,
    required String message,
    required String type,
    String? relatedId,
    String? relatedType,
    String? excludeUserId,
  }) async {
    try {
      final snap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('users')
          .where('role', isEqualTo: role)
          .get();
      for (final doc in snap.docs) {
        if (doc.id == excludeUserId) continue;
        await sendNotification(
          recipientId: doc.id,
          title: title,
          message: message,
          type: type,
          relatedId: relatedId,
          relatedType: relatedType,
        );
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}

FILE: lib\receiver_home_page.dart

import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'my_pickup_page.dart';
import 'receiver_profile_page.dart';
import 'receiver_map_page.dart';
import 'notification_service.dart';
import 'notifications_page.dart';
import 'widgets/ai_urgency_badge.dart';
import 'dart:async';

class ReceiverHomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const ReceiverHomePage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<ReceiverHomePage> createState() => _HomePageState();
}

class _HomePageState extends State<ReceiverHomePage> {
  // 'All' means no filter; others match posts' 'category' field
  String selectedCategory = "All";
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> categories = [
    {"name": "All", "icon": Icons.grid_view},
    {"name": "Vegetables", "icon": Icons.eco},
    {"name": "Meals", "icon": Icons.restaurant},
    {"name": "Bakery", "icon": Icons.bakery_dining},
    {"name": "Dairy", "icon": Icons.local_drink},
  ];
  String userName = "User";
  bool isLoading = true;
  int selectedIndex = 0;
  String searchQuery = "";
  // PHASE 6 FIX: No continuous location stream — removed excessive geolocator writes.
  // Location is only fetched on demand when user opens map tab.

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchUserName();
    // PHASE 6 FIX: No initState geolocator stream.
  }

  // PHASE 6 FIX: Removed _startLocationTracking() and _handleLocationPermission().
  // Continuous geolocator writes to Firestore on every 10m movement caused
  // excessive Firestore write costs and battery drain without any real benefit.
  // Location is read by MapPage on demand instead.

  @override
  void dispose() {
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ================= FETCH USER =================
  Future<void> _fetchUserName() async {
    final userDoc = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').doc(widget.userId);
    final docSnap = await userDoc.get();

    if (docSnap.exists) {
      setState(() {
        userName = docSnap.data()?['name'] ?? "User";
        isLoading = false;
      });
    } else {
      // Recreate user document if it was deleted
      await userDoc.set({
        "name": "User",
        "email": "",
        "role": "Receiver",
        "createdAt": FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
      setState(() => isLoading = false);
    }
  }



  // ================= SEND REQUEST =================
  Future<void> _sendRequest(FoodItem item) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("requests").add({
        "donorId": item.donorId,
        "ngoId": item.ngoId ?? '',  // populated if post is NGO-claimed
        "receiverId": widget.userId,
        "receiverName": userName,
        "donorName": item.donorName,
        "itemId": item.id,
        "itemTitle": item.title,
        "requestedItem": item.title,
        "itemImage": item.imageUrl,
        "status": "pending",
        "timestamp": FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      // PHASE 1 FIX: Notify NGO if post is claimed, otherwise notify Donor.
      if (item.ngoId != null && item.ngoId!.isNotEmpty) {
        await NotificationService.sendNotification(
          recipientId: item.ngoId!,
          title: "New Receiver Request!",
          message: "$userName wants '${item.title}' from your managed donation.",
          type: "request",
          relatedId: item.id,
          relatedType: 'post',
        );
      } else {
        await NotificationService.sendNotification(
          recipientId: item.donorId,
          title: "New Food Request!",
          message: "$userName has requested your '${item.title}'.",
          type: "request",
          relatedId: item.id,
          relatedType: 'post',
        );
      }

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('Food request sent successfully! 🚀'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF2E7D32),
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            duration: Duration(seconds: 3),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error sending request: $e");
      if (mounted) {
        // PHASE 4 FIX: Error catch must show error message, not success message.
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Failed to send request: $e'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.red,
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  // ================= NAVIGATION =================
  void _onTabTapped(int index) {
    setState(() {
      selectedIndex = index;
    });
  }

  Widget _getPage() {
    switch (selectedIndex) {
      case 0:
        return _homePage();
      case 1:
        return MyPickupsPage(userId: widget.userId);
      case 2:
        return ReceiverMapPage(userId: widget.userId);
      case 3:
        return ReceiverProfilePage(userId: widget.userId);
      default:
        return _homePage();
    }
  }

  // ================= HOME UI =================
  Widget _homePage() {
    // PHASE 1 FIX: Show BOTH 'active' posts AND 'claimed' NGO-managed posts.
    // Combine two Firestore streams client-side.
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('posts')
          .where('status', whereIn: ['active', 'claimed'])
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (!snapshot.hasData) {
          return CustomScrollView(
            slivers: [
              SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildHeader()),
              const SliverFillRemaining(
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green)),
              ),
            ],
          );
        }

        final allItems = snapshot.data!.docs.map((doc) {
          final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
          return FoodItem(
            id: doc.id,
            donorId: data['userID'] ?? '',
            donorName: data['donorName'] ?? 'Donor',
            donorImage: data['donorAvatar'] ?? '',
            ngoId: data['ngoId'],
            title: data['title'] ?? '',
            subtitle: data['description'] ?? '',
            imageUrl: data['imageUrl'] ?? '',
            distance: 0,
            serves: (data['quantity'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1,
            timeRange: data['expiryDate'] ?? '',
            status: data['status'] ?? 'active',
            category: data['category'] as String?,
            createdAt: data['createdAt'] as Timestamp?,
            // AI fields
            aiFreshnessStatus: data['aiFreshnessStatus'] as String?,
            aiUrgencyLevel: data['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String?,
            aiFreshnessScore: (data['aiFreshnessScore'] as num?)?.toInt(),
            estimatedRemainingHours: (data['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
            postPriority: data['postPriority'] as String?,
            aiScanEnabled: data['aiScanEnabled'] as bool? ?? false,
          );
        }).toList();

        // AI-Priority sort:
        //  1. urgency (critical > urgent > normal)
        //  2. freshness score ascending (lower = more urgent)
        //  3. estimated remaining hours ascending
        //  4. createdAt descending
        int urgencyRank(String? u) {
          if (u == 'critical') return 0;
          if (u == 'urgent') return 1;
          return 2;
        }
        allItems.sort((a, b) {
          final uCmp = urgencyRank(a.aiUrgencyLevel).compareTo(urgencyRank(b.aiUrgencyLevel));
          if (uCmp != 0) return uCmp;
          final sCmp = (a.aiFreshnessScore ?? 100).compareTo(b.aiFreshnessScore ?? 100);
          if (sCmp != 0) return sCmp;
          final hCmp = (a.estimatedRemainingHours ?? 99).compareTo(b.estimatedRemainingHours ?? 99);
          if (hCmp != 0) return hCmp;
          if (a.createdAt == null || b.createdAt == null) return 0;
          return b.createdAt!.compareTo(a.createdAt!);
        });

        // Apply search + category filter
        final displayedItems = allItems.where((item) {
          final matchesSearch = item.title.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
              item.subtitle.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
          // PHASE 2 FIX: Category filter now works against the 'category' field
          final matchesCategory = selectedCategory == 'All' ||
              (item.category?.toLowerCase() == selectedCategory.toLowerCase());
          return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
        }).toList();

        return CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildHeader()),
            SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildCategoryList()),
            SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildUpcomingEvents()),
            if (displayedItems.isEmpty)
              const SliverFillRemaining(
                child: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    "No food available matching your search.\nCheck back later!",
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 16),
                  ),
                ),
              )
            else
              SliverPadding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                sliver: SliverList(
                  delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                    (context, index) {
                      return FoodCardModern(
                        item: displayedItems[index],
                        onRequest: () => _sendRequest(displayedItems[index]),
                      );
                    },
                    childCount: displayedItems.length,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 50, 20, 30),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [Color(0xFF2E7D32), Color(0xFF66BB6A)],
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(40),
          bottomRight: Radius.circular(40),
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: Offset(0, 5),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    "Hello, $userName 👋",
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white70,
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 5),
                  const Text(
                    "Find Food \nNear You",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 28,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      height: 1.2,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              Row(
                children: [
                  _buildNotificationBell(),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(
                          color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1),
                          blurRadius: 8,
                        )
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: CircleAvatar(
                      radius: 26,
                      backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade100,
                      child: Text(
                        userName.isNotEmpty ? userName[0].toUpperCase() : '?',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 22,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.green,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 25),
          // Glassmorphism Search Bar
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            child: BackdropFilter(
              filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.3)),
                ),
                child: TextField(
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    setState(() {
                      searchQuery = value;
                    });
                  },
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    hintText: "Search for food, bakery...",
                    hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
                    border: InputBorder.none,
                    icon: Icon(Icons.search, color: Colors.white),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCategoryList() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 24, bottom: 10),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
            child: Text(
              "Categories",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          SizedBox(
            height: 55,
            child: ListView.builder(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              itemCount: categories.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final cat = categories[index];
                final isSelected = selectedCategory == cat["name"];
                return GestureDetector(
                  onTap: () => setState(() => selectedCategory = cat["name"]),
                  child: AnimatedContainer(
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 12),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: isSelected ? Colors.green : Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
                      boxShadow: [
                        if (isSelected)
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.3),
                            blurRadius: 8,
                            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                          )
                        else
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.04),
                            blurRadius: 4,
                            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                          ),
                      ],
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: isSelected ? Colors.green : Colors.grey.shade200,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          cat["icon"],
                          color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.green,
                          size: 20,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text(
                          cat["name"],
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.black87,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildUpcomingEvents() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
            child: Text(
              "Upcoming Food Drives",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection("drives")
                .where("status", isEqualTo: "active")
                .snapshots(),
            builder: (context, snapshot) {
              if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));
              }
              if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                return const Text("No upcoming events at the moment.", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey));
              }
              final drives = snapshot.data!.docs;
              return Column(
                children: drives.map((doc) {
                  final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                  final name = data['name'] ?? "Food Drive";
                  final location = data['location'] ?? "NGO Location";
                  final imageUrl = data['imageUrl'] ?? "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593113598332-cd288d649433?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=800";
                  return Container(
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05), blurRadius: 10, offset: const Offset(0, 5))],
                    ),
                    child: ListTile(
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      leading: ClipRRect(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        child: Image.network(imageUrl, width: 60, height: 60, fit: BoxFit.cover, errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const Icon(Icons.event, size: 40)),
                      ),
                      title: Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      subtitle: Text(location, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                      trailing: ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: () {
                           ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text("Drive details coming soon!"), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
                        },
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                        ),
                        child: const Text("View", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }).toList(),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _modernBottomNav() {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
          topLeft: Radius.circular(24),
          topRight: Radius.circular(24),
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.08),
            blurRadius: 20,
            offset: const Offset(0, -5),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: BottomNavigationBar(
        currentIndex: selectedIndex,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        selectedItemColor: Colors.green,
        unselectedItemColor: Colors.grey[400],
        showUnselectedLabels: true,
        selectedFontSize: 12,
        unselectedFontSize: 12,
        onTap: _onTabTapped,
        items: [
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.home_outlined, selectedIndex == 0),
            label: "Home",
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.shopping_bag_outlined, selectedIndex == 1),
            label: "Pickups",
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.map_outlined, selectedIndex == 2),
            label: "Map",
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.person_outline, selectedIndex == 3),
            label: "Profile",
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNotificationBell() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('notifications')
          .where('recipientId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .where('isRead', isEqualTo: false)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        bool hasUnread = snapshot.hasData && snapshot.data!.docs.isNotEmpty;

        return Stack(
          children: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined, color: Colors.white, size: 28),
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => NotificationsPage(userId: widget.userId)),
                );
              },
            ),
            if (hasUnread)
              Positioned(
                right: 8,
                top: 8,
                child: Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 12, minHeight: 12),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _bottomIcon(IconData icon, bool isSelected) {
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        gradient: isSelected
            ? const LinearGradient(colors: [Colors.green, Color(0xff66BB6A)])
            : null,
        color: isSelected ? null : Colors.transparent,
        boxShadow: isSelected
            ? [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.4),
                  blurRadius: 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                ),
              ]
            : null,
      ),
      child: Icon(icon, color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.grey[500]),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF8FAFC), // Modern off-white background
      body: _getPage(),
      bottomNavigationBar: _modernBottomNav(),
    );
  }
}

// ================= MODEL =================
class FoodItem {
  final String id;
  final String donorId;
  final String donorName;
  final String donorImage;
  final String? ngoId;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final String imageUrl;
  final double distance;
  final int serves;
  final String timeRange;
  final String status;
  final String? category;  // PHASE 2: category filtering
  final Timestamp? createdAt;
  // ── AI fields ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  final String? aiFreshnessStatus;     // 'fresh'|'moderate'|'expiringSoon'|'unsafe'
  final String? aiUrgencyLevel;        // 'normal'|'urgent'|'critical'
  final int? aiFreshnessScore;         // 0–100 (lower = more urgent for sorting)
  final double? estimatedRemainingHours;
  final String? postPriority;          // 'normal'|'urgent'
  final bool aiScanEnabled;

  FoodItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.donorId,
    required this.donorName,
    required this.donorImage,
    this.ngoId,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.imageUrl,
    required this.distance,
    required this.serves,
    required this.timeRange,
    required this.status,
    this.category,
    this.createdAt,
    this.aiFreshnessStatus,
    this.aiUrgencyLevel,
    this.aiFreshnessScore,
    this.estimatedRemainingHours,
    this.postPriority,
    this.aiScanEnabled = false,
  });
}

// ================= CARD =================
class FoodCardModern extends StatelessWidget {
  final FoodItem item;
  final VoidCallback onRequest;

  const FoodCardModern({required this.item, required this.onRequest, super.key});

  bool _isExpired(String time) {
    try {
      final expiry = DateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm a").parse(time);
      return DateTime.now().isAfter(expiry);
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final expired = _isExpired(item.timeRange);

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 15,
            offset: const Offset(0, 8),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // IMAGE & BADGES
          Stack(
            children: [
              ClipRRect(
                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
                child: Image.network(
                  item.imageUrl,
                  height: 180,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Container(
                    height: 180,
                    width: double.infinity,
                    color: Colors.grey[200],
                    child: const Icon(Icons.fastfood, size: 50, color: Colors.grey),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              // Serves badge
              Positioned(
                top: 16,
                right: 16,
                child: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  child: BackdropFilter(
                    filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 5, sigmaY: 5),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.5),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.people, color: Colors.white, size: 14),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Text(
                            'Serves ${item.serves}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 12),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              // NGO Managed badge
              if (item.status == 'claimed' || (item.ngoId != null && item.ngoId!.isNotEmpty))
                Positioned(
                  top: 16,
                  left: 16,
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.blue.shade700,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                    ),
                    child: const Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.business, color: Colors.white, size: 12),
                        SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text(
                          'NGO Managed',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Colors.white,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontSize: 11,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              // AI Urgency badge (top-left, below NGO badge when both present)
              if (item.aiScanEnabled && item.aiUrgencyLevel != null && item.aiUrgencyLevel != 'normal')
                Positioned(
                  bottom: 12,
                  left: 12,
                  child: AiUrgencyBadge(urgency: item.aiUrgencyLevel),
                ),
              // Expired Badge overlay
              if (expired)
                Positioned.fill(
                  child: Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
                      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
                    ),
                    child: const Center(
                      child: Text(
                        "EXPIRED",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 24,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          letterSpacing: 2,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          
          // CONTENT
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        item.title,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 20,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Color(0xFF1F2937),
                        ),
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Text(
                      expired ? "Expired" : "Active",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: expired ? Colors.red : Colors.green,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 14,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  item.subtitle,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 14,
                    color: Colors.grey[600],
                    height: 1.4,
                  ),
                  maxLines: 2,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                
                // DONOR INFO & TIME + AI Badges
                Row(
                  children: [
                    CircleAvatar(
                      radius: 14,
                      backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade100,
                      child: const Icon(Icons.person, size: 16, color: Colors.green),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        item.donorName,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Icon(Icons.access_time, size: 14, color: Colors.grey[500]),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        item.timeRange,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: Colors.grey[600],
                        ),
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                // AI badges row
                if (item.aiScanEnabled) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      if (item.aiFreshnessStatus != null)
                        AiFreshnessBadge(status: item.aiFreshnessStatus, compact: true),
                      if (item.estimatedRemainingHours != null && item.estimatedRemainingHours! > 0) ...[
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        AiCountdownBadge(remainingHours: item.estimatedRemainingHours, compact: true),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
                
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                
                // BUTTON
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  height: 50,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: expired ? null : onRequest,
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: item.status == 'claimed' ? Colors.blue.shade700 : Colors.green,
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                      elevation: expired ? 0 : 4,
                      shadowColor: Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      expired
                          ? 'Not Available'
                          : item.status == 'claimed'
                              ? 'Request (NGO Managed)'
                              : 'Request Pickup',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 16,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class ReceiverMapPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const ReceiverMapPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<ReceiverMapPage> createState() => _ReceiverMapPageState();
}

class _ReceiverMapPageState extends State<ReceiverMapPage> {
  Position? _currentPosition;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  final MapController _mapController = MapController();
  List<LatLng> _routePoints = [];
  LatLng? _volunteerPos;
  String? _volunteerName;
  String _deliveryStatus = "assigned"; // "assigned" or "picked_up"
  double _distanceRemaining = 0.0;
  double _volunteerHeading = 0.0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initializeLocation();
    _listenToActiveDelivery();
  }

  Future<void> _initializeLocation() async {
    try {
      Position position = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
        locationSettings: const LocationSettings(timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 5)),
      );
      if (mounted) setState(() { _currentPosition = position; _isLoading = false; });
    } catch (e) { 
      debugPrint("Location Error: $e");
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false); 
    }
  }

  void _listenToActiveDelivery() {
    // 1. Listen for NGO/Volunteer Delivery Tasks
    FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection("tasks")
        .where("receiverId", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
        .where("status", isEqualTo: "pending")
        .snapshots()
        .listen((snapshot) {
      if (snapshot.docs.isNotEmpty) {
        final taskData = snapshot.docs.first.data();
        final volunteerId = taskData['assignedTo'] as String?;
        final currentStatus = taskData['deliveryStatus'] ?? "assigned";
        
        if (mounted) setState(() => _deliveryStatus = currentStatus);

        if (volunteerId != null && volunteerId.isNotEmpty) {
          _listenToPersonLocation(volunteerId, "Rider");
        }
      }
    });

    // 2. Listen for Direct Donor-Receiver Accepted Requests
    FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection("requests")
        .where("receiverId", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
        .where("status", isEqualTo: "accepted")
        .snapshots()
        .listen((snapshot) {
      if (snapshot.docs.isNotEmpty) {
        final reqData = snapshot.docs.first.data();
        final donorId = reqData['donorId'] as String?;
        
        if (mounted) setState(() => _deliveryStatus = "donor_pickup");

        if (donorId != null && donorId.isNotEmpty) {
          _listenToPersonLocation(donorId, reqData['donorName'] ?? "Donor");
        }
      } else if (_deliveryStatus == "donor_pickup") {
        // If it was a donor pickup and now there are no accepted requests, clear it
        if (mounted) setState(() { _volunteerPos = null; _routePoints = []; });
      }
    });
  }

  void _listenToPersonLocation(String personId, String defaultName) {
    FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(personId).snapshots().listen((userSnap) {
      if (userSnap.exists && mounted) {
        final geo = userSnap.data()?['lastLocation'] as GeoPoint?;
        if (geo != null) {
          setState(() {
            _volunteerPos = LatLng(geo.latitude, geo.longitude);
            _volunteerName = userSnap.data()?['name'] ?? defaultName;
            _volunteerHeading = (userSnap.data()?['heading'] ?? 0.0).toDouble();
          });
          
          if (_currentPosition != null) {
            _fetchRoute(_volunteerPos!, LatLng(_currentPosition!.latitude, _currentPosition!.longitude));
            double dist = Geolocator.distanceBetween(_volunteerPos!.latitude, _volunteerPos!.longitude, _currentPosition!.latitude, _currentPosition!.longitude);
            setState(() => _distanceRemaining = dist / 1000);
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _fetchRoute(LatLng start, LatLng end) async {
    try {
      final url = 'https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/driving/${start.longitude},${start.latitude};${end.longitude},${end.latitude}?overview=full&geometries=geojson';
      final res = await http.get(Uri.parse(url));
      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
        final data = json.decode(res.body);
        final List coords = data['routes'][0]['geometry']['coordinates'];
        if (mounted) setState(() => _routePoints = coords.map((c) => LatLng(c[1], c[0])).toList());
      }
    } catch (e) { debugPrint("Route error: $e"); }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          _isLoading ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) : FlutterMap(
            mapController: _mapController,
            options: MapOptions(
              initialCenter: _currentPosition != null ? LatLng(_currentPosition!.latitude, _currentPosition!.longitude) : const LatLng(30.3753, 69.3451),
              initialZoom: 15.0,
            ),
            children: [
              TileLayer(
                urlTemplate: 'https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/rastertiles/voyager/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',
                subdomains: const ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'],
                retinaMode: RetinaMode.isHighDensity(context),
              ),
              if (_routePoints.isNotEmpty) PolylineLayer(polylines: [
                Polyline(points: _routePoints, strokeWidth: 10.0, color: Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.5)), // Thick Blue Glow
                Polyline(points: _routePoints, strokeWidth: 4.0, color: Colors.blueAccent), // Core Blue Line
              ]),
              MarkerLayer(markers: [
                if (_currentPosition != null) Marker(point: LatLng(_currentPosition!.latitude, _currentPosition!.longitude), width: 60, height: 60, child: const Icon(Icons.home_work, color: Colors.green, size: 40)),
                if (_volunteerPos != null) Marker(
                  point: _volunteerPos!,
                  width: 70, height: 70,
                  child: GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () {
                      _mapController.move(_volunteerPos!, 16.0);
                    },
                    child: Transform.rotate(
                      angle: _volunteerHeading * (3.14159 / 180),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.navigation, color: Color(0xFFD70F64), size: 35),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ]),
            ],
          ),

          // Receiver Status Banner
          if (_volunteerPos != null) Positioned(
            top: 50, left: 20, right: 20,
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFF2E7D32), 
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), 
                boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 10)]
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.directions_run, color: Colors.white, size: 30),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        "Pickup Mode Active", 
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)
                      ),
                      Text("You are ${_distanceRemaining.toStringAsFixed(1)} km away from the Donor", style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12)),
                    ],
                  )),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),

          // Bottom Info Card
          if (_volunteerPos != null) Positioned(
            bottom: 20, left: 16, right: 16,
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24), boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black12, blurRadius: 20)]),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      CircleAvatar(
                        radius: 25, 
                        backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade50, 
                        child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.green)
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 16),
                      Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                        Text(_volunteerName ?? "Donor", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 18)),
                        const Text("Destination: Food Pickup Location", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 13)),
                      ])),
                      IconButton(onPressed: () {}, icon: const Icon(Icons.phone, color: Colors.green)),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const Divider(height: 20),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () {
                            _mapController.move(_volunteerPos!, 17.0);
                          },
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location),
                          label: const Text("Locate Donor"),
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.grey[200],
                            foregroundColor: Colors.black87,
                            elevation: 0,
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () {
                             // Center on user
                             if (_currentPosition != null) {
                               _mapController.move(LatLng(_currentPosition!.latitude, _currentPosition!.longitude), 16.0);
                             }
                          },
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.navigation),
                          label: const Text("My Location"),
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                            elevation: 0,
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}



================================================================================
