import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'notifications_page.dart';
import 'notification_service.dart';
import 'volunteer_available_tasks_page.dart';
import 'volunteer_profile_page.dart';
import 'map_page.dart';

class VolunteerDashboardPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const VolunteerDashboardPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<VolunteerDashboardPage> createState() => _VolunteerDashboardPageState();
}

class _VolunteerDashboardPageState extends State<VolunteerDashboardPage> {
  int selectedIndex = 0;
  bool isLoading = true;
  String volunteerName = "";
  String volunteerEmail = "";
  int pendingTasks = 0;
  int completedTasks = 0;

  // Profile editing
  bool isEditingProfile = false;
  final nameEditCtrl = TextEditingController();
  bool isSavingProfile = false;
  
  StreamSubscription<QuerySnapshot>? _taskSubscription;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadDashboardData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _taskSubscription?.cancel();
    nameEditCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadDashboardData() async {
    try {
      final userDoc = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(widget.userId);
      final userSnap = await userDoc.get();
      if (userSnap.exists) {
        volunteerName = userSnap['name'] ?? "Volunteer";
        volunteerEmail = userSnap.data()?['email'] ?? "";
        nameEditCtrl.text = volunteerName;
      }

      // We'll set up listeners for real-time stats instead of one-time get
      _taskSubscription = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection("tasks")
          .where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .snapshots()
          .listen((snapshot) {
        int pending = 0;
        int completed = 0;
        for (var doc in snapshot.docs) {
          final status = doc.data()['status'] as String?;
          if (status == 'pending' || status == 'assigned' || status == 'in_progress') pending++;
          if (status == 'delivered') completed++;
        }
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            pendingTasks = pending;
            completedTasks = completed;
            isLoading = false;
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error loading dashboard data: $e");
      if (mounted) setState(() => isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _markTaskCompleted(String taskId) async {
    try {
      final taskDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("tasks").doc(taskId).get();
      await taskDoc.reference.update({"status": "delivered"});

      if (taskDoc.exists) {
        final data = taskDoc.data();
        if (data != null) {
          if (data.containsKey("requestId") && data["requestId"] != null) {
            final reqId = data["requestId"];
            await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("requests").doc(reqId).update({"status": "completed"});
          }
          if (data.containsKey("postId") && data["postId"] != null) {
            final postId = data["postId"];
            await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("posts").doc(postId).update({"status": "completed"});
          }
          if (data.containsKey("donorId") && data["donorId"] != null) {
            await NotificationService.sendNotification(
              recipientId: data["donorId"],
              title: "Delivery Completed!",
              message: "Your food donation has been successfully delivered.",
              type: "delivery",
              relatedId: taskId,
            );
          }
          if (data.containsKey("receiverId") && data["receiverId"] != null) {
            await NotificationService.sendNotification(
              recipientId: data["receiverId"],
              title: "Food Delivered!",
              message: "Your requested food has arrived.",
              type: "delivery",
              relatedId: taskId,
            );
          }
        }
      }

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text("Task marked as completed! Great job!"), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Error marking task completed: $e");
    }
  }

  // ---------------- UI COMPONENTS ----------------

  Widget _buildTaskCard(DocumentSnapshot task, {bool showComplete = true}) {
    final data = task.data() as Map<String, dynamic>?;
    final title = data?['title'] ?? "Delivery Task";
    final pickup = data?['pickup'] ?? "Unknown Pickup";
    final dropoff = data?['dropoff'] ?? "Unknown Drop-off";
    final estTime = data?['estTime'] ?? "N/A";
    final status = data?['status'] ?? "pending";
    final bool isDone = status == "delivered";

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: isDone ? Colors.green.withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.orange.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 20,
            offset: const Offset(0, 10),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  colors: isDone
                      ? [Colors.green.shade50, Colors.green.shade100]
                      : [Colors.orange.shade50, Colors.orange.shade100],
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      isDone ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.delivery_dining,
                      color: isDone ? Colors.green : Colors.orange,
                      size: 24,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          title,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.black87),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          isDone ? "Delivery Completed" : "NGO Assigned Task",
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            color: isDone ? Colors.green.shade700 : Colors.orange.shade700,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  _buildLocationRow(Icons.storefront, "Pickup", pickup),
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 11, top: 4, bottom: 4),
                    child: Align(
                      alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                      child: Container(width: 2, height: 16, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                    ),
                  ),
                  _buildLocationRow(Icons.location_on, "Drop-off", dropoff),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.access_time, size: 16, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          Text(estTime, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade700, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                        ],
                      ),
                      if (!isDone && showComplete)
                        ElevatedButton(
                          onPressed: () => _markTaskCompleted(task.id),
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                            elevation: 0,
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 12),
                          ),
                          child: const Text("Mark Complete", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLocationRow(IconData icon, String label, String address) {
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Icon(icon, size: 24, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(label, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 2),
              Text(address, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // Pages ...

  // ---------------- PAGES ----------------

  Widget _dashboardPage() {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Hero Banner
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 60, 24, 40),
            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [Color(0xFF2E7D32), Color(0xFF66BB6A)],
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              ),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(bottomLeft: Radius.circular(40), bottomRight: Radius.circular(40)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Text("Welcome back,", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 16)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        isLoading
                            ? Container(width: 150, height: 28, color: Colors.white24)
                            : Text(volunteerName, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 28, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    _buildNotificationBell(),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2), shape: BoxShape.circle),
                      child: CircleAvatar(
                        radius: 26,
                        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                        child: Text(
                          volunteerName.isNotEmpty ? volunteerName[0].toUpperCase() : "V",
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.green),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 30),
                // Glassmorphism Stats
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                  child: BackdropFilter(
                    filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.15),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                      children: [
                        StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("tasks").where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId).where("status", whereIn: ["pending", "assigned", "in_progress"]).snapshots(),
                          builder: (context, snap) {
                            final count = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
                            return _heroStat(count.toString(), "Pending");
                          },
                        ),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 40, color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                        StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("tasks").where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId).where("status", isEqualTo: "delivered").snapshots(),
                          builder: (context, snap) {
                            final count = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
                            return _heroStat(count.toString(), "Completed");
                          },
                        ),
                        Container(width: 1, height: 40, color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                        StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("tasks").where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId).snapshots(),
                          builder: (context, snap) {
                            final count = snap.hasData ? snap.data!.docs.length : 0;
                            return _heroStat(count.toString(), "Total");
                          },
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const Text("Recent Activity", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.black87)),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
                  stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                      .collection("tasks")
                      .where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
                      .limit(5)
                      .snapshots(),
                  builder: (context, snapshot) {
                    if (!snapshot.hasData) {
                      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));
                    }
                    if (snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                      return Center(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                          child: Column(
                            children: [
                              Icon(Icons.inbox_outlined, size: 64, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                              const SizedBox(height: 16),
                              Text("No tasks yet", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                              const SizedBox(height: 8),
                              Text("The NGO will assign deliveries to you soon.", textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    }
                    // Sort client-side to avoid composite index requirement
                    final docs = List.of(snapshot.data!.docs);
                    docs.sort((a, b) {
                      final aT = (a.data() as Map)['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
                      final bT = (b.data() as Map)['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
                      if (aT == null || bT == null) return 0;
                      return bT.compareTo(aT);
                    });
                    return Column(
                      children: docs.take(3).map((doc) => _buildTaskCard(doc)).toList(),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildNotificationBell() {
    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('notifications')
          .where('recipientId', isEqualTo: widget.userId)
          .where('isRead', isEqualTo: false)
          .snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        bool hasUnread = snapshot.hasData && snapshot.data!.docs.isNotEmpty;

        return Stack(
          children: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined, color: Colors.white, size: 28),
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => NotificationsPage(userId: widget.userId)),
                );
              },
            ),
            if (hasUnread)
              Positioned(
                right: 8,
                top: 8,
                child: Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                  constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 12, minHeight: 12),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _heroStat(String value, String label) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.white)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: Colors.white70, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
      ],
    );
  }



  Widget _bottomIcon(IconData icon, bool isSelected) {
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isSelected ? Colors.green.withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.transparent,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Icon(icon),
    );
  }

  Widget _modernBottomNav() {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(topLeft: Radius.circular(24), topRight: Radius.circular(24)),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.08), blurRadius: 20, offset: const Offset(0, -5))],
      ),
      child: BottomNavigationBar(
        currentIndex: selectedIndex,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        selectedItemColor: Colors.green,
        unselectedItemColor: Colors.grey.shade400,
        showUnselectedLabels: true,
        selectedFontSize: 12,
        unselectedFontSize: 12,
        onTap: (index) => setState(() => selectedIndex = index),
        items: [
          BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.home_outlined, selectedIndex == 0), label: "Home"),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.assignment_outlined, selectedIndex == 1), label: "Tasks"),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.map_outlined, selectedIndex == 2), label: "Map"),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: _bottomIcon(Icons.person_outline, selectedIndex == 3), label: "Profile"),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _getPage(int index) {
    switch (index) {
      case 0: return _dashboardPage();
      case 1: return VolunteerAvailableTasksPage(
        userId: widget.userId,
        onTabSwitch: (newIndex) => setState(() => selectedIndex = newIndex),
      );
      case 2: return MapPage(userId: widget.userId);
      case 3: return VolunteerProfilePage(userId: widget.userId);
      default: return _dashboardPage();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF7F9FC),
      body: _getPage(selectedIndex),
      bottomNavigationBar: _modernBottomNav(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'login_screen.dart';

class VolunteerProfilePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;

  const VolunteerProfilePage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<VolunteerProfilePage> createState() => _VolunteerProfilePageState();
}

class _VolunteerProfilePageState extends State<VolunteerProfilePage> {
  final TextEditingController _nameController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isEditing = false;
  bool _isSaving = false;
  bool _initialized = false; // prevent controller reset on every stream rebuild

  Future<void> _logout() async {
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: const Text("Logout", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: const Text("Are you sure you want to log out?"),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text("Cancel", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: Colors.red,
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            ),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text("Logout", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirm == true) {
      await FirebaseAuth.instance.signOut();
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginScreen()),
          (route) => false,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _updateProfile() async {
    final newName = _nameController.text.trim();
    if (newName.isEmpty) return;

    setState(() => _isSaving = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection("users")
          .doc(widget.userId)
          .update({"name": newName});
      setState(() {
        _isEditing = false;
      });
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text("Profile updated!"), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text("Failed to update profile."), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isSaving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xffF7F9FC),
      body: StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection("users").doc(widget.userId).snapshots(),
        builder: (context, userSnapshot) {
          if (!userSnapshot.hasData) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green));

          final userData = userSnapshot.data!.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
          final String name = userData['name'] ?? "Volunteer";
          final String email = userData['email'] ?? "";

          if (!_initialized) {
            _initialized = true;
            _nameController.text = name;
          } else if (!_isEditing) {
            _nameController.text = name;
          }

          return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                .collection("tasks")
                .where("assignedTo", isEqualTo: widget.userId)
                .snapshots(),
            builder: (context, tasksSnapshot) {
              int completed = 0;
              int assigned = 0;
              if (tasksSnapshot.hasData) {
                for (var doc in tasksSnapshot.data!.docs) {
                  final s = doc['status'] ?? '';
                  if (s == 'completed' || s == 'delivered') completed++;
                  if (s == 'assigned' || s == 'pending') assigned++;
                }
              }

              return SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    // Hero Section
                    Stack(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          height: 250,
                          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                            gradient: LinearGradient(
                              colors: [Color(0xFF2E7D32), Color(0xFF66BB6A)],
                              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                            ),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                              bottomLeft: Radius.circular(40),
                              bottomRight: Radius.circular(40),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SafeArea(
                          child: Column(
                            children: [
                              const SizedBox(height: 20),
                              const Center(
                                child: Text(
                                  "Profile",
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontSize: 24,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 30),
                              Center(
                                child: Stack(
                                  alignment: Alignment.bottomRight,
                                  children: [
                                    Container(
                                      width: 120,
                                      height: 120,
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 4),
                                        boxShadow: [
                                          BoxShadow(
                                            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                            blurRadius: 20,
                                            offset: const Offset(0, 10),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                      child: CircleAvatar(
                                        radius: 56,
                                        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                                        child: Text(
                                          name.isNotEmpty ? name[0].toUpperCase() : "V",
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            fontSize: 48,
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                            color: Colors.green,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    GestureDetector(
                                      onTap: () => setState(() => _isEditing = !_isEditing),
                                      child: Container(
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                                        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                          color: Colors.white,
                                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                        ),
                                        child: Icon(
                                          _isEditing ? Icons.close : Icons.edit,
                                          size: 20,
                                          color: Colors.green,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),

                    const SizedBox(height: 20),

                    // Name and Email
                    if (!_isEditing) ...[
                      Text(
                        name,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 26,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.black87,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        email,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 16,
                          color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ] else ...[
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 40),
                        child: Column(
                          children: [
                            TextField(
                              controller: _nameController,
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 24,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              ),
                              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                                hintText: "Enter your name",
                                border: InputBorder.none,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            ElevatedButton(
                              onPressed: _isSaving ? null : _updateProfile,
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: _isSaving
                                  ? const SizedBox(
                                      width: 20,
                                      height: 20,
                                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                        strokeWidth: 2,
                                      ),
                                    )
                                  : const Text(
                                      "Save Name",
                                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                                    ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],

                    const SizedBox(height: 30),

                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          _buildStatCard("Completed", completed, Colors.green, Icons.check_circle),
                          const SizedBox(width: 16),
                          _buildStatCard("Assigned", assigned, Colors.orange, Icons.pending_actions),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),

                    const SizedBox(height: 30),

                    // Actions List
                    Container(
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                        boxShadow: [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                            blurRadius: 20,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          _buildActionTile(
                            Icons.person_outline,
                            "Account Details",
                            email,
                            () => showDialog(
                              context: context,
                              builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
                                title: const Text("Account Details"),
                                content: Column(
                                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text("Name: $name"),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                    Text("Email: $email"),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                    const Text("Role: Volunteer"),
                                  ],
                                ),
                                actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text("Close"))],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Divider(height: 1, color: Colors.grey.shade100),
                          _buildActionTile(
                            Icons.notifications_none,
                            "Notifications",
                            "Real-time task alerts",
                            () => ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(content: Text("Notifications are enabled for task assignments."), backgroundColor: Colors.green),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Divider(height: 1, color: Colors.grey.shade100),
                          _buildActionTile(
                            Icons.help_outline,
                            "Support",
                            "contact@leftoverfood.app",
                            () => showDialog(
                              context: context,
                              builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
                                title: const Text("Support"),
                                content: const Text("For help, email us at:\ncontact@leftoverfood.app"),
                                actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text("Close"))],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Divider(height: 1, color: Colors.grey.shade100),
                          _buildActionTile(
                            Icons.logout,
                            "Logout",
                            "Sign out of your account",
                            _logout,
                            isDestructive: true,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 40),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatCard(String title, int count, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              blurRadius: 20,
              offset: const Offset(0, 10),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 30),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              count.toString(),
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 24,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              title,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionTile(IconData icon, String title, String subtitle, VoidCallback onTap, {bool isDestructive = false}) {
    return ListTile(
      onTap: onTap,
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
      leading: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red.shade50 : Colors.green.shade50,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        ),
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red : Colors.green,
          size: 24,
        ),
      ),
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          color: isDestructive ? Colors.red : Colors.black87,
        ),
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        subtitle,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 12,
          color: Colors.grey.shade500,
        ),
      ),
      trailing: Icon(
        Icons.chevron_right,
        color: Colors.grey.shade400,
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// App-wide configuration. Override at build time with --dart-define.
class AppConfig {
  AppConfig._();

  static const String appName = 'FoodShare';
  static const String tagline = 'Sharing Is Caring';

  static const String geminiApiKey = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GEMINI_API_KEY',
    defaultValue: 'AIzaSyBy2JgA4aaE_zjLT1oxCyWXUMQD54dHkYw',
  );

  static const String cloudinaryCloudName = String.fromEnvironment(
    'CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME',
    defaultValue: 'dtcrhkwxv',
  );

  static const String cloudinaryUploadPreset = String.fromEnvironment(
    'CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET',
    defaultValue: 'fyp_posts_preset',
  );

  static bool get hasGeminiKey =>
      geminiApiKey.isNotEmpty && !geminiApiKey.startsWith('REPLACE_');
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppTheme {
  AppTheme._();

  static const Color primaryGreen = Color(0xFF2E7D32);
  static const Color primaryDark = Color(0xFF1B5E20);
  static const Color accent = Color(0xFF43A047);
  static const Color textPrimary = Color(0xFF1F2C37);
  static const Color textMuted = Color(0xFF7D8FAB);
  static const Color surface = Color(0xFFF8F8F8);
  static const Color card = Colors.white;

  static ThemeData get light {
    final base = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
      seedColor: primaryGreen,
      brightness: Brightness.light,
      primary: primaryGreen,
      secondary: accent,
    );

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      colorScheme: base,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: surface,
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: primaryGreen,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: card,
        elevation: 0,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: primaryGreen,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, 50),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: const Color(0xFFF4F6FA),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        labelStyle: const TextStyle(color: textMuted),
      ),
      snackBarTheme: SnackBarThemeData(
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: const BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        selectedItemColor: primaryGreen,
        unselectedItemColor: textMuted,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
      floatingActionButtonTheme: const FloatingActionButtonThemeData(
        backgroundColor: primaryGreen,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: lib\models\food_analysis_model.dart

/// AI Food Analysis Result Model — v2
///
/// Extended with:
///   • aiUrgencyLevel  (normal / urgent / critical)
///   • aiFreshnessScore (0–100 numeric for sorting)
///   • estimatedRemainingHours (how many hours food is safe)
///   • postPriority    (normal / urgent)
///   • aiModerationPassed (image is real food + usable quality)
library;

enum FreshnessStatus { fresh, moderate, expiringSoon, unsafe }

enum ExpiryRisk { safeToDonate, donateQuickly, unsafeToConsume }

enum AiUrgencyLevel { normal, urgent, critical }

enum FoodCategory {
  fruits,
  vegetables,
  cookedMeals,
  bakery,
  dairy,
  meat,
  beverages,
  other,
}

class FoodAnalysisResult {
  final FreshnessStatus freshnessStatus;
  final ExpiryRisk expiryRisk;
  final FoodCategory foodCategory;
  final AiUrgencyLevel urgencyLevel;

  /// 0.0 – 1.0 confidence from AI (displayed as percentage)
  final double confidence;

  /// 0–100 freshness score for numeric sorting (100 = perfectly fresh)
  final int freshnessScore;

  /// Estimated hours the food remains safe for consumption
  final double estimatedRemainingHours;

  /// Whether the AI scan was actually performed (vs. skipped/failed)
  final bool scanEnabled;

  /// true = image is real food AND meets quality bar (not blurry/dark/non-food)
  final bool moderationPassed;

  /// Human-readable safety recommendation
  final String safetyRecommendation;

  /// Timestamp of when analysis was performed
  final DateTime analyzedAt;

  /// Optional raw notes from the AI model for debugging
  final String? rawNotes;

  /// The specific name/type of food detected by AI (e.g., "Apple", "Pizza")
  final String? foodName;

  /// Whether the AI determined the image contains food
  final bool? isFood;

  const FoodAnalysisResult({
    required this.freshnessStatus,
    required this.expiryRisk,
    required this.foodCategory,
    required this.urgencyLevel,
    required this.confidence,
    required this.freshnessScore,
    required this.estimatedRemainingHours,
    required this.scanEnabled,
    required this.moderationPassed,
    required this.safetyRecommendation,
    required this.analyzedAt,
    this.rawNotes,
    this.foodName,
    this.isFood,
  });

  // ─── Firestore serialization ──────────────────────────────────────────────

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    return {
      'aiFreshnessStatus': freshnessStatus.name,
      'aiExpiryRisk': expiryRisk.name,
      'aiFoodCategory': foodCategory.name,
      'aiUrgencyLevel': urgencyLevel.name,
      'aiConfidence': confidencePercent,
      'aiFreshnessScore': freshnessScore,
      'estimatedRemainingHours': estimatedRemainingHours,
      'postPriority': urgencyLevel == AiUrgencyLevel.normal ? 'normal' : 'urgent',
      'aiModerationPassed': moderationPassed,
      'aiSafetyRecommendation': safetyRecommendation,
      'aiScanEnabled': scanEnabled,
      'aiAnalyzedAt': analyzedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      if (foodName != null) 'aiFoodName': foodName,
      if (isFood != null) 'aiIsFood': isFood,
    };
  }

  factory FoodAnalysisResult.fromFirestore(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    return FoodAnalysisResult(
      freshnessStatus: FreshnessStatus.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == data['aiFreshnessStatus'],
        orElse: () => FreshnessStatus.moderate,
      ),
      expiryRisk: ExpiryRisk.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == data['aiExpiryRisk'],
        orElse: () => ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly,
      ),
      foodCategory: FoodCategory.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == data['aiFoodCategory'],
        orElse: () => FoodCategory.other,
      ),
      urgencyLevel: AiUrgencyLevel.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == data['aiUrgencyLevel'],
        orElse: () => AiUrgencyLevel.normal,
      ),
      confidence: ((data['aiConfidence'] ?? 0) as num) / 100.0,
      freshnessScore: (data['aiFreshnessScore'] ?? 50) as int,
      estimatedRemainingHours:
          ((data['estimatedRemainingHours'] ?? 0) as num).toDouble(),
      scanEnabled: data['aiScanEnabled'] ?? false,
      moderationPassed: data['aiModerationPassed'] ?? true,
      safetyRecommendation: data['aiSafetyRecommendation'] ?? '',
      analyzedAt: DateTime.tryParse(data['aiAnalyzedAt'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      foodName: data['aiFoodName'],
      isFood: data['aiIsFood'],
    );
  }

  // ─── Display helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  String get freshnessLabel {
    switch (freshnessStatus) {
      case FreshnessStatus.fresh:
        return 'Fresh';
      case FreshnessStatus.moderate:
        return 'Moderate';
      case FreshnessStatus.expiringSoon:
        return 'Expiring Soon';
      case FreshnessStatus.unsafe:
        return 'Unsafe';
    }
  }

  String get expiryRiskLabel {
    switch (expiryRisk) {
      case ExpiryRisk.safeToDonate:
        return 'Safe for Donation';
      case ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly:
        return 'Donate Quickly';
      case ExpiryRisk.unsafeToConsume:
        return 'Unsafe to Consume';
    }
  }

  String get urgencyLabel {
    switch (urgencyLevel) {
      case AiUrgencyLevel.normal:
        return 'Normal';
      case AiUrgencyLevel.urgent:
        return 'Urgent';
      case AiUrgencyLevel.critical:
        return 'Critical';
    }
  }

  String get foodCategoryLabel {
    switch (foodCategory) {
      case FoodCategory.fruits:
        return 'Fruits';
      case FoodCategory.vegetables:
        return 'Vegetables';
      case FoodCategory.cookedMeals:
        return 'Cooked Meals';
      case FoodCategory.bakery:
        return 'Bakery';
      case FoodCategory.dairy:
        return 'Dairy';
      case FoodCategory.meat:
        return 'Meat';
      case FoodCategory.beverages:
        return 'Beverages';
      case FoodCategory.other:
        return 'Other';
    }
  }

  int get confidencePercent => (confidence * 100).round();

  /// Returns true when food is unsafe — upload MUST be blocked
  bool get isUnsafe =>
      freshnessStatus == FreshnessStatus.unsafe ||
      expiryRisk == ExpiryRisk.unsafeToConsume ||
      !moderationPassed;

  /// Returns true when donor should see a caution warning (non-blocking)
  bool get requiresWarning =>
      freshnessStatus == FreshnessStatus.expiringSoon ||
      expiryRisk == ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly ||
      confidence < 0.5;

  String get countdownLabel {
    if (estimatedRemainingHours <= 0) return 'Expired';
    if (estimatedRemainingHours < 1) {
      return '${(estimatedRemainingHours * 60).round()} min left';
    }
    return '${estimatedRemainingHours.toStringAsFixed(0)}h left';
  }
}

/// AiAssistantService — Phase 2 Placeholder
///
/// Modular architecture for future AI chatbot integration.
/// Currently provides:
///   • Structured message types ready for LLM routing
///   • Context-aware prompt builder stubs
///   • Role-specific assistant personalities (Donor / Receiver / NGO / Volunteer)
///   • Integration hooks for Gemini / OpenAI / Dialogflow
///
/// To activate: replace _mockResponse() with a real API call.
library;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Message types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

enum AssistantRole {
  donor,
  receiver,
  ngo,
  volunteer,
}

enum MessageType {
  text,
  suggestion,     // clickable quick-reply chip
  foodGuidance,   // food safety tip card
  navigationHint, // volunteer route suggestion
  escalation,     // urgent action required
}

class AssistantMessage {
  final String content;
  final MessageType type;
  final bool isFromUser;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final List<String> suggestions; // quick reply chips

  AssistantMessage({
    required this.content,
    this.type = MessageType.text,
    this.isFromUser = false,
    this.suggestions = const [],
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) : timestamp = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Service
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiAssistantService {
  final AssistantRole role;
  final List<AssistantMessage> _history = [];

  AiAssistantService({required this.role});

  List<AssistantMessage> get history => List.unmodifiable(_history);

  // ── SEND MESSAGE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Send a user message and receive an AI response.
  /// Replace [_mockResponse] with a real LLM call.
  Future<AssistantMessage> send(String userMessage) async {
    final userMsg = AssistantMessage(
      content: userMessage,
      isFromUser: true,
      type: MessageType.text,
    );
    _history.add(userMsg);

    final response = await _mockResponse(userMessage);
    _history.add(response);
    return response;
  }

  // ── CONTEXT-AWARE PROMPT BUILDER ──────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Builds a system prompt personalized to the user's role.
  /// This will be prepended to every LLM API call.
  String buildSystemPrompt() {
    const base = 'You are an AI assistant for the LeftOver Food App, '
        'a community food rescue platform. Be concise, compassionate, and helpful. '
        'Always prioritize food safety and community well-being.';

    return switch (role) {
      AssistantRole.donor => '$base You are helping a DONOR. Guide them on '
          'food safety, proper donation practices, and how to post food donations '
          'correctly. Discourage unsafe food donations.',
      AssistantRole.receiver => '$base You are helping a RECEIVER. Help them '
          'find available food, understand pickup instructions, and make requests. '
          'Be sensitive and respectful.',
      AssistantRole.ngo => '$base You are helping an NGO COORDINATOR. Provide '
          'insights on donation management, volunteer coordination, and optimizing '
          'food distribution efficiency.',
      AssistantRole.volunteer => '$base You are helping a VOLUNTEER. Guide them '
          'on task acceptance, safe food handling during delivery, navigation, '
          'and reporting issues.',
    };
  }

  // ── ROLE-SPECIFIC QUICK STARTERS ─────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Returns contextual quick-reply suggestions based on role.
  List<String> getQuickSuggestions() {
    return switch (role) {
      AssistantRole.donor => [
          'How do I post food safely?',
          'What food can I donate?',
          'How does AI check my food?',
          'Can I cancel a post?',
        ],
      AssistantRole.receiver => [
          'How do I request food?',
          'Where is my pickup?',
          'What food is available now?',
          'Update my food preferences',
        ],
      AssistantRole.ngo => [
          'How do I claim a donation?',
          'Assign volunteer to task',
          'Show urgent donations',
          'View AI analytics',
        ],
      AssistantRole.volunteer => [
          'Show my tasks',
          'How do I deliver safely?',
          'Mark task as delivered',
          'Report a problem',
        ],
    };
  }

  // ── FOOD SAFETY GUIDANCE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Returns a food-safety tip card for a given food category.
  /// TODO: Replace with dynamic Gemini call for personalized tips.
  static AssistantMessage getFoodSafetyTip(String category) {
    const tips = <String, String>{
      'meat':
          '🥩 Meat must be kept below 4°C at all times. Donate only if cooked or freshly stored. Never donate if more than 2 days old after cooking.',
      'dairy':
          '🥛 Dairy spoils quickly. Check for off-smell or texture changes. Donate only if sealed and within expiry date.',
      'cookedMeals':
          '🍱 Cooked meals are high-risk. Donate within 2–3 hours of cooking. Keep hot foods above 60°C during transport.',
      'fruits':
          '🍎 Fresh fruit is generally safe. Remove visibly moldy pieces. Store separately from cooked items.',
      'bakery':
          '🍞 Bakery items are safe for 1–2 days. Avoid if mold is visible. Keep dry and sealed.',
      'vegetables':
          '🥦 Vegetables are safe if firm and brightly colored. Wilted or mushy is borderline — donate quickly.',
    };

    return AssistantMessage(
      content: tips[category] ?? '🌱 Always ensure donated food is fresh, properly stored, and within safe consumption time.',
      type: MessageType.foodGuidance,
      suggestions: ['Tell me more', 'Check other categories', 'Post a donation'],
    );
  }

  // ── MOCK RESPONSE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// PLACEHOLDER: Replace with real Gemini / OpenAI API call.
  ///
  /// TODO: Implement _realGeminiChat(userMessage, history)
  /// TODO: Implement _dialogflowSession()
  /// TODO: Implement voice input / TTS output for accessibility
  Future<AssistantMessage> _mockResponse(String input) async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 800));
    final lower = input.toLowerCase();

    if (lower.contains('food') && lower.contains('safe')) {
      return AssistantMessage(
        content: 'Great question! Our AI scans every donated food image for freshness. '
            'If food is flagged as unsafe, the upload is automatically blocked to protect recipients.',
        suggestions: ['Learn more', 'Post a donation'],
        type: MessageType.foodGuidance,
      );
    }
    if (lower.contains('urgent') || lower.contains('expir')) {
      return AssistantMessage(
        content: '⚡ Urgent donations are automatically escalated to NGOs and sorted to the top of receiver feeds. '
            'Volunteers are notified immediately.',
        suggestions: ['Show urgent posts', 'Contact NGO'],
        type: MessageType.escalation,
      );
    }
    return AssistantMessage(
      content: 'I\'m here to help! You can ask me about food donations, '
          'pickup instructions, AI safety checks, or volunteer tasks.',
      suggestions: getQuickSuggestions().take(3).toList(),
    );
  }
}

FILE: lib\services\ai_food_service.dart
