/// AI Food Freshness & Safety Detection Service — v3.1
///
/// Changes from v3:
///   • Removed unsupported responseMimeType (caused silent 400 errors)
///   • Per-stage soft-fail: failure in any stage uses safe defaults, pipeline continues
///   • Retry-once on HTTP errors
///   • Full debugPrint logging per stage (timing + raw response + parse result)
///   • Image validation before sending (size, null check)
///   • Robust JSON extraction (strips markdown fences, extracts first {...} block)
///   • Consolidated to 2 Gemini calls (recognition+spoilage merged, freshness separate)
///     to reduce latency and rate-limit exposure
library;

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../core/app_config.dart';
import '../models/food_analysis_model.dart';

class AiFoodService {
  static String get _apiKey => AppConfig.geminiApiKey;
  // gemini-1.5-flash is deprecated and removed. Using gemini-flash-latest instead.
  static const String _model = 'gemini-flash-latest';
  static const String _apiVersion = 'v1beta'; // Keep on v1beta for now, it's safer for newer models
  static const bool hardBlockUnsafe = true;

  // ── Cache ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  static final Map<int, FoodAnalysisResult> _cache = {};
  static void clearCache() => _cache.clear();

  // ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Future<FoodAnalysisResult> analyze({Uint8List? imageBytes}) async {
    if (imageBytes == null || imageBytes.isEmpty) {
      debugPrint('[AI] ❌ analyze() called with null/empty imageBytes');
      return _errorFallback('No image provided');
    }
    debugPrint('[AI] 📷 Image size: ${imageBytes.lengthInBytes} bytes');

    // FIX 1: Content-based hash (not identity hash — Dart's .hashCode on
    // Uint8List is identity-based and changes every time for same content).
    final hash = _contentHash(imageBytes);
    if (_cache.containsKey(hash)) {
      debugPrint('[AI] 💾 Cache hit');
      return _cache[hash]!;
    }

    // Images >800KB take >35s to upload on slow web connections — warn early.
    if (imageBytes.lengthInBytes > 819200) {
      debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Large image ${imageBytes.lengthInBytes ~/ 1024}KB — may be slow on web. Consider compressing to <500KB.');
    }
    // Hard limit at 4MB (Gemini inline limit)
    if (imageBytes.lengthInBytes > 4194304) {
      debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Image too large (${imageBytes.lengthInBytes} bytes) — returning cautious fallback');
      return _largeImageFallback();
    }

    // FIX 3: Detect real MIME type from magic bytes.
    final mime = _detectMime(imageBytes);
    debugPrint('[AI] 🖼️ Detected MIME: $mime');

    // FIX 4: Offload CPU-heavy base64 encoding to an isolate to prevent UI freezing
    final b64 = await compute(base64Encode, imageBytes);
    debugPrint('[AI] 🔐 Base64 length: ${b64.length} chars');

    final result = await _runPipeline(b64, mime);
    
    // Save to cache and enforce LRU limit of 5 to prevent OOM
    _cache[hash] = result;
    if (_cache.length > 5) {
      _cache.remove(_cache.keys.first);
    }
    
    return result;
  }

  /// Stable content hash: sample 128 bytes evenly across the image.
  static int _contentHash(Uint8List bytes) {
    if (bytes.length <= 128) {
      return bytes.fold(17, (h, b) => h * 31 + b);
    }
    final step = bytes.length ~/ 128;
    int h = 17;
    for (int i = 0; i < 128; i++) {
      h = h * 31 + bytes[i * step];
    }
    return h;
  }

  /// Detect MIME type from file magic bytes.
  static String _detectMime(Uint8List bytes) {
    if (bytes.length >= 4) {
      // PNG: \x89PNG
      if (bytes[0] == 0x89 && bytes[1] == 0x50 && bytes[2] == 0x4E && bytes[3] == 0x47) {
        return 'image/png';
      }
      // JPEG: \xFF\xD8
      if (bytes[0] == 0xFF && bytes[1] == 0xD8) return 'image/jpeg';
      // WebP: RIFF....WEBP
      if (bytes.length >= 12 &&
          bytes[0] == 0x52 && bytes[1] == 0x49 && // RI
          bytes[8] == 0x57 && bytes[9] == 0x45) {  // WE
        return 'image/webp';
      }
      // GIF
      if (bytes[0] == 0x47 && bytes[1] == 0x49 && bytes[2] == 0x46) return 'image/gif';
      // HEIC/HEIF (ftyp box)
      if (bytes.length >= 12 && bytes[4] == 0x66 && bytes[5] == 0x74 && bytes[6] == 0x79 && bytes[7] == 0x70) {
        return 'image/heic';
      }
    }
    // Default: Gemini accepts image/jpeg as fallback
    return 'image/jpeg';
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // PIPELINE (soft-fail per stage)
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Future<FoodAnalysisResult> _runPipeline(String b64, String mime) async {
    // ── STAGE 1+2: Recognition + Spoilage (single call) ──────────────────
    final t1 = DateTime.now();
    Map<String, dynamic> s12 = {};
    try {
      s12 = await _callStage12(b64, mime);
      final ms = DateTime.now().difference(t1).inMilliseconds;
      debugPrint('[AI] ✅ Stage 1+2 OK in ${ms}ms → ${s12.toString().substring(0, s12.toString().length.clamp(0, 200))}');
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Stage 1+2 FAILED: $e — using defaults');
    }

    // If image is not food → hard block immediately
    final isFood = s12['isFood'] as bool? ?? true; // default true on failure
    if (s12.containsKey('isFood') && !isFood) {
      final reason = s12['moderationReason'] as String? ?? 'Not food';
      debugPrint('[AI] 🚫 Stage 1: Not food — $reason');
      return _nonFoodResult(reason);
    }

    final cat = s12['category'] as String? ?? 'other';
    final catConf = (s12['categoryConfidence'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.5;
    final spoilageScore = (s12['overallSpoilageScore'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 30;
    final moldVisible = s12['moldVisible'] as bool? ?? false;
    final moldSev = s12['moldSeverity'] as String? ?? 'none';
    final liquidLeakage = s12['liquidLeakage'] as bool? ?? false;
    final rottingSpots = s12['rottingSpots'] as bool? ?? false;
    final spoilageEvidence = s12['spoilageEvidence'] as String? ?? '';
    final imageOk = s12['isImageClear'] as bool? ?? true;

    if (!imageOk) debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Stage 1: Image quality poor');

    // ── STAGE 3: Freshness Score (TEXT-ONLY — no second image upload) ────────
    final foodName = s12['foodName'] as String? ?? cat;
    final t3 = DateTime.now();
    Map<String, dynamic> s3 = {};
    try {
      s3 = await _callStage3TextOnly(cat, spoilageScore, moldSev, foodName, spoilageEvidence);
      final ms = DateTime.now().difference(t3).inMilliseconds;
      debugPrint('[AI] ✅ Stage 3 OK in ${ms}ms → $s3');
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Stage 3 FAILED: $e — deriving from spoilage score');
      s3 = _deriveFromSpoilage(spoilageScore);
    }

    var freshnessStr = s3['freshnessStatus'] as String? ?? 'moderate';
    var freshnessScore = (s3['freshnessScore'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 50;
    final confidence = (s3['confidence'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.5;

    debugPrint('[AI] 📊 Pre-override: freshness=$freshnessStr score=$freshnessScore conf=$confidence');

    // ── STAGE 4: Shelf Life (local) ───────────────────────────────────────
    final shelf = _stageShelfLife(cat, freshnessStr, freshnessScore);
    var expiryRisk = shelf['expiryRisk'] as String;
    var remainHours = (shelf['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num).toDouble();
    debugPrint('[AI] ⏱️ Stage 4: $cat → ${remainHours}h, risk=$expiryRisk');

    // ── STAGE 5: Rule overrides + synthesize ─────────────────────────────
    // Rule 1: Any visible mold → minimum expiringSoon
    if (moldVisible && freshnessStr == 'fresh') {
      freshnessStr = 'expiringSoon';
      freshnessScore = freshnessScore.clamp(0, 45);
      debugPrint('[AI] 🔒 Override R1: mold visible → expiringSoon');
    }
    // Rule 2: Moderate/severe mold → unsafe
    if (moldSev == 'severe' || moldSev == 'moderate') {
      freshnessStr = 'unsafe';
      freshnessScore = freshnessScore.clamp(0, 15);
      expiryRisk = 'unsafeToConsume';
      remainHours = 0;
      debugPrint('[AI] 🔒 Override R2: mold $moldSev → unsafe');
    }
    // Rule 3: Liquid leakage on meat/dairy → unsafe
    if (liquidLeakage && (cat == 'meat' || cat == 'dairy')) {
      freshnessStr = 'unsafe';
      freshnessScore = freshnessScore.clamp(0, 10);
      expiryRisk = 'unsafeToConsume';
      remainHours = 0;
      debugPrint('[AI] 🔒 Override R3: liquid leakage on $cat → unsafe');
    }
    // Rule 4: Rotting spots on fresh → expiringSoon
    if (rottingSpots && freshnessStr == 'fresh') {
      freshnessStr = 'expiringSoon';
      freshnessScore = freshnessScore.clamp(0, 48);
      debugPrint('[AI] 🔒 Override R4: rotting spots → expiringSoon');
    }
    // Rule 5: High spoilage score
    if (spoilageScore > 70) {
      freshnessStr = 'unsafe';
      freshnessScore = freshnessScore.clamp(0, 19);
      expiryRisk = 'unsafeToConsume';
      remainHours = 0;
      debugPrint('[AI] 🔒 Override R5: spoilageScore=$spoilageScore → unsafe');
    }
    // Rule 6: Low confidence → moderate/uncertain (don't fake precision)
    final lowConf = confidence < 0.55 || catConf < 0.50;
    if (lowConf && freshnessStr != 'unsafe') {
      debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Low confidence (conf=$confidence catConf=$catConf) → capping at moderate');
      freshnessStr = 'moderate';
      freshnessScore = 50;
      expiryRisk = 'donateQuickly';
      remainHours = remainHours.clamp(0, 6);
    }

    final urgency = switch (freshnessStr) {
      'unsafe' || 'expiringSoon' => AiUrgencyLevel.critical,
      'moderate' => AiUrgencyLevel.urgent,
      _ => AiUrgencyLevel.normal,
    };

    final rec = _buildRecommendation(
      cat: cat,
      freshnessStr: freshnessStr,
      lowConf: lowConf,
      moldSev: moldSev,
      remainHours: remainHours,
    );

    final finalResult = FoodAnalysisResult(
      freshnessStatus: _toFreshnessStatus(freshnessStr),
      expiryRisk: _toExpiryRisk(expiryRisk),
      foodCategory: _toFoodCategory(cat),
      urgencyLevel: urgency,
      confidence: lowConf ? 0.45 : confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
      freshnessScore: freshnessScore.clamp(0, 100),
      estimatedRemainingHours: remainHours,
      scanEnabled: true,
      moderationPassed: freshnessStr != 'unsafe',
      safetyRecommendation: rec,
      analyzedAt: DateTime.now(),
      rawNotes: spoilageEvidence.isNotEmpty ? spoilageEvidence : 'stage12=${s12.isNotEmpty}',
      foodName: foodName,
      isFood: true,
    );

    debugPrint('[AI] ✅ Final: $freshnessStr score=$freshnessScore hours=${remainHours}h urgency=${urgency.name}');
    return finalResult;
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // GEMINI CALL: Stage 1+2 — Recognition + Spoilage (merged into ONE call)
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _callStage12(String b64, String mime) async {
    const prompt = r'''
You are a food safety AI. Analyze this food image carefully.
Respond with ONLY a JSON object — no markdown, no explanation, no code fences.

Return exactly this structure:
{
  "isFood": true,
  "isImageClear": true,
  "moderationReason": "",
  "foodName": "banana",
  "category": "fruits",
  "categoryConfidence": 0.92,
  "moldVisible": false,
  "moldSeverity": "none",
  "discoloration": false,
  "discolorationSeverity": "none",
  "textureDegradation": false,
  "liquidLeakage": false,
  "rottingSpots": false,
  "overallSpoilageScore": 5,
  "spoilageEvidence": "vibrant yellow color, firm texture"
}

Field rules:
- isFood: false if image shows person, object, text, blank, screenshot
- isImageClear: false if image is too blurry or dark to analyze
- category must be one of: fruits, vegetables, cookedMeals, bakery, dairy, meat, beverages, other
- categoryConfidence: your certainty about category (0.0–1.0)
- moldSeverity: none | trace | moderate | severe
- discolorationSeverity: none | slight | moderate | severe
- overallSpoilageScore: 0=perfectly fresh, 100=completely rotten
- spoilageEvidence: brief list of what you see (max 80 chars)

Spoilage scoring guide:
0–10: perfectly fresh, no issues
11–30: minor natural variation, safe
31–50: moderate aging, some deterioration
51–70: significant spoilage, donate quickly
71–100: severe spoilage, unsafe
''';

    final raw = await _geminiCall(prompt, b64, mime: mime, stage: '1+2');
    final parsed = _extractJson(raw);
    if (parsed == null) throw Exception('JSON parse failed: $raw');
    return parsed;
  }

  // Stage 3 is TEXT-ONLY — does not resend the image (avoids double-upload
  // timeout). It uses the structured data from Stage 1+2 to infer freshness.
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _callStage3TextOnly(
      String cat, int spoilageScore, String moldSev,
      String foodName, String spoilageEvidence) async {
    final prompt = '''
You are a food freshness scorer. Analyze the following data about a food item.
Respond with ONLY a JSON object — no markdown, no explanation.

Food data:
- Name: $foodName
- Category: $cat
- Spoilage score: $spoilageScore/100
- Mold severity: $moldSev
- Observed evidence: $spoilageEvidence

Return exactly this structure:
{
  "freshnessStatus": "fresh",
  "freshnessScore": 88,
  "colorHealth": 90,
  "textureHealth": 85,
  "confidence": 0.82
}

Rules:
- freshnessStatus: fresh | moderate | expiringSoon | unsafe
- If spoilageScore > 70 → unsafe, freshnessScore < 20
- If spoilageScore 40–70 → at most expiringSoon
- If moldSeverity moderate/severe → unsafe
- If spoilageScore < 30 and no mold → fresh or moderate
- confidence < 0.55 → set freshnessStatus=moderate, freshnessScore=50

Freshness guide for $cat:
${_freshnessGuide(cat)}
''';

    // Text-only call — no image data, no mime type needed
    final url = Uri.parse(
      'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/$_apiVersion/models/$_model:generateContent?key=$_apiKey',
    );
    final body = jsonEncode({
      'contents': [
        {'parts': [{'text': prompt}]},
      ],
      'generationConfig': {'temperature': 0.05, 'maxOutputTokens': 2048},
    });

    debugPrint('[AI] 📡 Stage 3 (text-only) — no image re-upload...');
    Exception? lastError;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
      try {
        final resp = await http
            .post(url, headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: body)
            .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 20));

        debugPrint('[AI] 📥 Stage 3 attempt $attempt — HTTP ${resp.statusCode}');

        if (resp.statusCode != 200) {
          lastError = Exception('HTTP ${resp.statusCode}: ${resp.body.substring(0, resp.body.length.clamp(0, 150))}');
          debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Stage 3 HTTP error: $lastError');
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue;
        }

        final decoded = jsonDecode(resp.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final candidates = decoded['candidates'] as List<dynamic>?;
        if (candidates == null || candidates.isEmpty) {
          lastError = Exception('empty candidates');
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue;
        }
        final text = candidates[0]['content']?['parts']?[0]?['text'] as String?;
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty) {
          lastError = Exception('empty text');
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue;
        }
        debugPrint('[AI] 📝 Stage 3 raw: ${text.substring(0, text.length.clamp(0, 200))}');
        final parsed = _extractJson(text);
        if (parsed != null) return parsed;
        lastError = Exception('JSON parse failed: $text');
      } on Exception catch (e) {
        lastError = e;
        debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Stage 3 attempt $attempt: $e');
        if (attempt < 3) {
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
        }
      }
    }
    throw lastError ?? Exception('Stage 3: all attempts failed');
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // GEMINI HTTP CALLER — with retry + full logging
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Future<String> _geminiCall(String prompt, String b64,
      {required String stage, String mime = 'image/jpeg'}) async {
    final url = Uri.parse(
      'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/$_apiVersion/models/$_model:generateContent?key=$_apiKey',
    );

    final body = jsonEncode({
      'contents': [
        {
          'parts': [
            {'inline_data': {'mime_type': mime, 'data': b64}},
            {'text': prompt},
          ],
        },
      ],
      'generationConfig': {'temperature': 0.05, 'maxOutputTokens': 4096},
    });

    debugPrint('[AI] 📡 Stage $stage — calling Gemini ($mime)...');
    Exception? lastError;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
      try {
        final resp = await http
            .post(url, headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, body: body)
            .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 35));

        debugPrint('[AI] 📥 Stage $stage attempt $attempt — HTTP ${resp.statusCode} (${resp.body.length} bytes)');

        if (resp.statusCode != 200) {
          lastError = Exception('HTTP ${resp.statusCode}: ${resp.body.substring(0, resp.body.length.clamp(0, 200))}');
          debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Stage $stage HTTP error: $lastError');
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue; // retry
        }

        // Parse candidates
        final decoded = jsonDecode(resp.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final candidates = decoded['candidates'] as List<dynamic>?;

        // Gemini blocked response (SAFETY / RECITATION)
        if (candidates == null || candidates.isEmpty) {
          final blockReason = decoded['promptFeedback']?['blockReason'] as String?;
          debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Stage $stage: empty candidates. blockReason=$blockReason');
          lastError = Exception('empty candidates: blockReason=$blockReason');
          // Safety blocks won't resolve on retry — break early
          if (blockReason != null) break;
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue;
        }

        // Check finish reason
        final finishReason = candidates[0]['finishReason'] as String?;
        if (finishReason == 'SAFETY') {
          debugPrint('[AI] ⚠️ Stage $stage blocked by SAFETY filter');
          throw Exception('Stage $stage: blocked by SAFETY filter');
        }

        final text = candidates[0]['content']?['parts']?[0]?['text'] as String?;
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty) {
          debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Stage $stage: null/empty text in response');
          lastError = Exception('empty text in response');
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
          continue;
        }

        debugPrint('[AI] 📝 Stage $stage raw[${text.length}]: ${text.substring(0, text.length.clamp(0, 250))}');
        return text;

      } on Exception catch (e) {
        lastError = e;
        debugPrint('[AI] ❌ Stage $stage attempt $attempt exception: $e');
        if (attempt < 3) {
          final backoff = (pow(2, attempt) * 1000).toInt() + Random().nextInt(1000);
          await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: backoff));
        }
      }
    }

    throw lastError ?? Exception('Stage $stage: all 3 attempts failed');
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // JSON EXTRACTION — strips markdown fences, finds first {...} block
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Map<String, dynamic>? _extractJson(String raw) {
    // Try 1: direct parse
    try {
      return jsonDecode(raw.trim()) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    } catch (_) {}

    // Try 2: strip markdown fences
    final stripped = raw
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'```json\s*', caseSensitive: false), '')
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'```\s*'), '')
        .trim();
    try {
      return jsonDecode(stripped) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    } catch (_) {}

    // Try 3: extract first {...} block via regex
    final match = RegExp(r'\{[\s\S]*\}').firstMatch(stripped);
    if (match != null) {
      try {
        return jsonDecode(match.group(0)!) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    debugPrint('[AI] ❌ _extractJson failed on: $stripped');
    return null;
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // STAGE 4 — Shelf Life (local, no API)
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  static Map<String, dynamic> _stageShelfLife(
      String cat, String status, int score) {
    const baselines = {
      'fruits':      {'fresh': 48.0, 'moderate': 24.0, 'expiringSoon': 6.0, 'unsafe': 0.0},
      'vegetables':  {'fresh': 72.0, 'moderate': 36.0, 'expiringSoon': 12.0, 'unsafe': 0.0},
      'cookedMeals': {'fresh': 6.0,  'moderate': 3.0,  'expiringSoon': 1.0,  'unsafe': 0.0},
      'bakery':      {'fresh': 48.0, 'moderate': 24.0, 'expiringSoon': 6.0,  'unsafe': 0.0},
      'dairy':       {'fresh': 24.0, 'moderate': 12.0, 'expiringSoon': 3.0,  'unsafe': 0.0},
      'meat':        {'fresh': 4.0,  'moderate': 2.0,  'expiringSoon': 0.5,  'unsafe': 0.0},
      'beverages':   {'fresh': 24.0, 'moderate': 12.0, 'expiringSoon': 4.0,  'unsafe': 0.0},
      'other':       {'fresh': 24.0, 'moderate': 12.0, 'expiringSoon': 4.0,  'unsafe': 0.0},
    };

    final catMap = baselines[cat] ?? baselines['other']!;
    final baseHours = catMap[status] ?? 4.0;

    // Scale hours within tier based on score
    final tierPct = switch (status) {
      'fresh'        => (score - 85).clamp(0, 15) / 15.0,
      'moderate'     => (score - 50).clamp(0, 34) / 34.0,
      'expiringSoon' => (score - 20).clamp(0, 29) / 29.0,
      _              => 0.0,
    };
    final hours = (baseHours * (0.7 + tierPct * 0.6)).clamp(0.0, 72.0);

    final expiryRisk = switch (status) {
      'unsafe'       => 'unsafeToConsume',
      'expiringSoon' => 'donateQuickly',
      'moderate'     => 'donateQuickly',
      _              => 'safeToDonate',
    };

    return {'estimatedRemainingHours': hours, 'expiryRisk': expiryRisk};
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // HELPERS
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Derive freshness tier from spoilage score when Stage 3 fails
  static Map<String, dynamic> _deriveFromSpoilage(int spoilageScore) {
    final String status;
    final int score;
    if (spoilageScore > 70) {
      status = 'unsafe';
      score = 10;
    } else if (spoilageScore > 50) {
      status = 'expiringSoon';
      score = 35;
    } else if (spoilageScore > 30) {
      status = 'moderate';
      score = 55;
    } else {
      status = 'fresh';
      score = 85;
    }
    return {'freshnessStatus': status, 'freshnessScore': score, 'confidence': 0.50};
  }

  static String _freshnessGuide(String cat) => switch (cat) {
    'fruits'      => 'fresh≥85: vibrant color, firm. moderate 50–84: minor blemishes. expiringSoon 20–49: heavy browning/soft. unsafe<20: mold/rot',
    'vegetables'  => 'fresh≥85: crisp vivid. moderate 50–84: slight wilt. expiringSoon 20–49: slimy patches. unsafe<20: rot/mold',
    'cookedMeals' => 'fresh≥85: looks just cooked. moderate 50–84: slightly dried. expiringSoon 20–49: discolored/dried. unsafe<20: mold/off-color',
    'meat'        => 'fresh≥85: pink/red firm. moderate 50–84: edge color change. expiringSoon 20–49: grey patches. unsafe<20: grey/slimy',
    'dairy'       => 'fresh≥85: normal color intact. moderate 50–84: slight shift. expiringSoon 20–49: separation. unsafe<20: mold/swelling',
    'bakery'      => 'fresh≥85: normal no spots. moderate 50–84: slightly stale. expiringSoon 20–49: very dry. unsafe<20: ANY mold spot',
    _             => 'fresh≥85: looks good. moderate 50–84: minor issues. expiringSoon 20–49: deteriorating. unsafe<20: clear spoilage',
  };

  static String _buildRecommendation({
    required String cat,
    required String freshnessStr,
    required bool lowConf,
    required String moldSev,
    required double remainHours,
  }) {
    if (lowConf) return 'AI uncertain — inspect manually before donating.';
    return switch (freshnessStr) {
      'unsafe'       => moldSev != 'none' ? 'Mold detected. Do not donate.' : 'Spoilage detected. Not safe for donation.',
      'expiringSoon' => 'Donate immediately — ~${remainHours.round()}h remaining.',
      'moderate'     => 'Donate within ${remainHours.round()}h. Keep refrigerated.',
      _              => 'Food looks fresh. Safe to donate (~${remainHours.round()}h).',
    };
  }

  static FoodAnalysisResult _nonFoodResult(String reason) => FoodAnalysisResult(
        freshnessStatus: FreshnessStatus.unsafe,
        expiryRisk: ExpiryRisk.unsafeToConsume,
        foodCategory: FoodCategory.other,
        urgencyLevel: AiUrgencyLevel.critical,
        confidence: 0.95,
        freshnessScore: 0,
        estimatedRemainingHours: 0,
        scanEnabled: true,
        moderationPassed: false,
        safetyRecommendation: reason.isNotEmpty ? 'Not food: $reason' : 'Image does not contain food.',
        analyzedAt: DateTime.now(),
        rawNotes: 'moderation_failed',
        isFood: false,
        foodName: 'Not Food',
      );

  static FoodAnalysisResult _errorFallback(String err) {
    debugPrint('[AI] 🔴 _errorFallback: $err');
    return FoodAnalysisResult(
      freshnessStatus: FreshnessStatus.moderate,
      expiryRisk: ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly,
      foodCategory: FoodCategory.other,
      urgencyLevel: AiUrgencyLevel.normal,
      confidence: 0.40,
      freshnessScore: 50,
      estimatedRemainingHours: 4,
      scanEnabled: false,
      moderationPassed: true,
      safetyRecommendation: 'AI scan failed — verify freshness manually.',
      analyzedAt: DateTime.now(),
      rawNotes: 'error: $err',
      isFood: null,
      foodName: null,
    );
  }

  static FoodAnalysisResult _largeImageFallback() => FoodAnalysisResult(
        freshnessStatus: FreshnessStatus.moderate,
        expiryRisk: ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly,
        foodCategory: FoodCategory.other,
        urgencyLevel: AiUrgencyLevel.urgent,
        confidence: 0.40,
        freshnessScore: 50,
        estimatedRemainingHours: 6,
        scanEnabled: false,
        moderationPassed: true,
        safetyRecommendation: 'Image too large for AI scan — verify freshness manually.',
        analyzedAt: DateTime.now(),
        rawNotes: 'image_too_large',
        isFood: null,
        foodName: null,
      );

  static FreshnessStatus _toFreshnessStatus(String s) =>
      FreshnessStatus.values.firstWhere((e) => e.name == s, orElse: () => FreshnessStatus.moderate);

  static ExpiryRisk _toExpiryRisk(String s) =>
      ExpiryRisk.values.firstWhere((e) => e.name == s, orElse: () => ExpiryRisk.donateQuickly);

  static FoodCategory _toFoodCategory(String s) =>
      FoodCategory.values.firstWhere((e) => e.name == s, orElse: () => FoodCategory.other);
}



================================================================================
FILE: lib\services\ai_moderation_service.dart

/// AiModerationService — Phase 2
///
/// Advanced moderation:
///   1. Duplicate image detection (perceptual hash comparison)
///   2. Suspicious spam-behaviour detection (flood detection)
///   3. Fraud risk scoring (0–100)
///   4. Moderation flag writing to Firestore
///
/// NOTE: Full perceptual-hash (pHash) requires the `image` package.
/// This implementation uses a lightweight byte-sample approach as a
/// production-ready approximation. Replace with a proper pHash or
/// Vision API call in a full production deployment.
library;

import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Result model
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ModerationResult {
  final bool passed;
  final int fraudRiskScore;           // 0–100 (higher = more suspicious)
  final List<String> moderationFlags; // human-readable flag labels
  final String summary;

  const ModerationResult({
    required this.passed,
    required this.fraudRiskScore,
    required this.moderationFlags,
    required this.summary,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() => {
        'moderationPassed': passed,
        'fraudRiskScore': fraudRiskScore,
        'moderationFlags': moderationFlags,
        'moderationSummary': summary,
        'moderationCheckedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Service
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiModerationService {
  static final _db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // Lightweight perceptual fingerprint cache: hash → first postId seen
  static final Map<int, String> _imageHashRegistry = {};

  // ── 1. FULL MODERATION CHECK ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Run all moderation checks on a new post before / after upload.
  ///
  /// [imageBytes]  : raw image bytes for duplicate detection
  /// [donorId]     : UID of the uploading user
  /// [title]       : post title (for spam keyword detection)
  static Future<ModerationResult> checkPost({
    required Uint8List imageBytes,
    required String donorId,
    required String title,
  }) async {
    final flags = <String>[];
    int riskScore = 0;

    // ── a. Duplicate image detection ─────────────────────────────────────
    final imgHash = _lightweightHash(imageBytes);
    if (_imageHashRegistry.containsKey(imgHash)) {
      flags.add('duplicate_image');
      riskScore += 40;
    } else {
      _imageHashRegistry[imgHash] = donorId;
    }

    // ── b. Flood detection (>3 posts in 10 minutes) ────────────────────
    final floodResult = await _checkFlood(donorId);
    if (floodResult) {
      flags.add('flood_detected');
      riskScore += 35;
    }

    // ── c. Spam keyword detection ─────────────────────────────────────
    if (_hasSpamKeywords(title)) {
      flags.add('spam_keywords');
      riskScore += 20;
    }

    // ── d. Account age check (new accounts = higher risk) ─────────────
    final newAccount = await _isNewAccount(donorId);
    if (newAccount) {
      flags.add('new_account');
      riskScore += 10;
    }

    riskScore = riskScore.clamp(0, 100);
    final passed = riskScore < 60 && !flags.contains('duplicate_image');

    String summary;
    if (flags.isEmpty) {
      summary = 'No moderation issues detected.';
    } else if (riskScore >= 60) {
      summary = 'High fraud risk: ${flags.join(", ")}. Flagged for NGO review.';
    } else {
      summary = 'Minor flags: ${flags.join(", ")}. Monitoring active.';
    }

    return ModerationResult(
      passed: passed,
      fraudRiskScore: riskScore,
      moderationFlags: flags,
      summary: summary,
    );
  }

  // ── 2. WRITE MODERATION RESULT TO FIRESTORE ───────────────────────────────

  /// Persist moderation result to the post document and optionally
  /// write to a 'moderationQueue' collection for NGO review.
  static Future<void> persistModerationResult({
    required String postId,
    required ModerationResult result,
  }) async {
    final batch = _db.batch();

    // Update the post
    batch.update(_db.collection('posts').doc(postId), result.toFirestore());

    // If high risk, add to NGO moderation queue
    if (!result.passed || result.fraudRiskScore >= 60) {
      final queueRef = _db.collection('moderationQueue').doc(postId);
      batch.set(queueRef, {
        'postId': postId,
        'fraudRiskScore': result.fraudRiskScore,
        'flags': result.moderationFlags,
        'summary': result.summary,
        'status': 'pending_review',
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    }

    await batch.commit();
  }

  // ── 3. GET FLAGGED POSTS FOR NGO REVIEW ──────────────────────────────────

  static Stream<QuerySnapshot> streamFlaggedPosts() {
    return _db
        .collection('moderationQueue')
        .where('status', isEqualTo: 'pending_review')
        .orderBy('createdAt', descending: true)
        .limit(20)
        .snapshots();
  }

  /// Mark a flagged post as reviewed by an NGO.
  static Future<void> markReviewed(String postId, {bool approve = false}) async {
    await _db.collection('moderationQueue').doc(postId).update({
      'status': approve ? 'approved' : 'rejected',
      'reviewedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'reviewedBy': FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? '',
    });
    if (!approve) {
      await _db.collection('posts').doc(postId).update({'status': 'rejected'});
    }
  }

  // ── PRIVATE HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Lightweight image fingerprint: sample 64 evenly-spaced bytes and hash.
  /// Not a true pHash but sufficient for exact-duplicate detection.
  static int _lightweightHash(Uint8List bytes) {
    if (bytes.isEmpty) return 0;
    const sampleCount = 64;
    final step = (bytes.length / sampleCount).floor().clamp(1, bytes.length);
    int hash = 17;
    for (int i = 0; i < sampleCount; i++) {
      final idx = (i * step).clamp(0, bytes.length - 1);
      hash = hash * 31 + bytes[idx];
    }
    return hash;
  }

  static Future<bool> _checkFlood(String donorId) async {
    try {
      final since = Timestamp.fromDate(
          DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(minutes: 10)));
      final snap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('userID', isEqualTo: donorId)
          .where('createdAt', isGreaterThan: since)
          .get();
      return snap.docs.length > 3;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  static bool _hasSpamKeywords(String title) {
    const spamWords = ['free money', 'click here', 'win', '!!!', 'buy now', '100% free'];
    final lower = title.toLowerCase();
    return spamWords.any((w) => lower.contains(w));
  }

  static Future<bool> _isNewAccount(String userId) async {
    try {
      final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
      if (user == null) return false;
      final created = user.metadata.creationTime;
      if (created == null) return false;
      return DateTime.now().difference(created).inDays < 3;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Clear the image hash registry (call on logout).
  static void clearRegistry() => _imageHashRegistry.clear();
}



================================================================================
FILE: lib\services\ai_monitoring_service.dart
================================================================================

/// AiMonitoringService — Phase 2
///
/// Real-time AI monitoring data for the NGO Live Dashboard.
/// All methods return streams or futures directly from Firestore —
/// no extra network calls, reads are minimal and batched.
library;

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Monitoring snapshot model
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MonitoringSnapshot {
  final int activeUrgentDonations;
  final int deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk;
  final int unsafeUploadsBlocked;
  final double avgDeliveryMinutes;
  final double foodWastePreventedKg;
  final double aiInterventionSuccessRate; // % of urgent posts that got claimed
  final DateTime computedAt;

  const MonitoringSnapshot({
    required this.activeUrgentDonations,
    required this.deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk,
    required this.unsafeUploadsBlocked,
    required this.avgDeliveryMinutes,
    required this.foodWastePreventedKg,
    required this.aiInterventionSuccessRate,
    required this.computedAt,
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Predictive analytics snapshot
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class PredictiveInsight {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final String trend; // '↑' | '↓' | '→'
  final String insight;

  const PredictiveInsight({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.trend,
    required this.insight,
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Service
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiMonitoringService {
  static final _db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // ── 1. LIVE MONITORING SNAPSHOT ───────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Computes the full monitoring snapshot for the NGO dashboard.
  /// Single read-burst: 4 Firestore queries, results cached for 60s.
  static DateTime? _lastComputed;
  static MonitoringSnapshot? _cachedSnapshot;

  static Future<MonitoringSnapshot> getMonitoringSnapshot() async {
    // 60-second cache
    if (_cachedSnapshot != null &&
        _lastComputed != null &&
        DateTime.now().difference(_lastComputed!).inSeconds < 60) {
      return _cachedSnapshot!;
    }

    try {
      // Q1: Active urgent/critical posts
      final urgentSnap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('status', whereIn: ['active', 'claimed'])
          .where('aiUrgencyLevel', whereIn: ['urgent', 'critical'])
          .get();

      // Q2: Tasks at spoilage risk
      final spoilageSnap = await _db
          .collection('tasks')
          .where('status', isEqualTo: 'assigned')
          .where('spoilageRiskDuringDelivery', whereIn: ['high', 'critical'])
          .get();

      // Q3: Unsafe blocked (posts with moderation failure or unsafe status)
      final blockedSnap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('aiModerationPassed', isEqualTo: false)
          .get();
      final unsafeSnap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('aiFreshnessStatus', isEqualTo: 'unsafe')
          .get();
      final totalBlocked = blockedSnap.docs.length + unsafeSnap.docs.length;

      // Q4: Completed tasks — compute average delivery time
      final completedSnap = await _db
          .collection('tasks')
          .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
          .limit(100)
          .get();
      double totalMins = 0;
      int withPrediction = 0;
      for (final doc in completedSnap.docs) {
        final mins =
            (doc.data()['predictedDeliveryMinutes'] as num?)?.toDouble();
        if (mins != null) {
          totalMins += mins;
          withPrediction++;
        }
      }
      final avgMins = withPrediction > 0 ? totalMins / withPrediction : 0.0;

      // Waste prevented estimate: each blocked unsafe post ≈ 3 kg saved
      final wasteKg = totalBlocked * 3.0;

      // AI intervention success rate:
      // % of urgent posts that were subsequently claimed
      final urgentTotal = urgentSnap.docs.length;
      final urgentClaimed = urgentSnap.docs.where((d) {
        final data = d.data();
        return data['status'] == 'claimed' || data['ngoId'] != null;
      }).length;
      final successRate =
          urgentTotal > 0 ? urgentClaimed / urgentTotal * 100 : 0.0;

      final snap = MonitoringSnapshot(
        activeUrgentDonations: urgentSnap.docs.length,
        deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk: spoilageSnap.docs.length,
        unsafeUploadsBlocked: totalBlocked,
        avgDeliveryMinutes: avgMins,
        foodWastePreventedKg: wasteKg,
        aiInterventionSuccessRate: successRate,
        computedAt: DateTime.now(),
      );
      _cachedSnapshot = snap;
      _lastComputed = DateTime.now();
      return snap;
    } catch (_) {
      return MonitoringSnapshot(
        activeUrgentDonations: 0,
        deliveriesAtSpoilageRisk: 0,
        unsafeUploadsBlocked: 0,
        avgDeliveryMinutes: 0,
        foodWastePreventedKg: 0,
        aiInterventionSuccessRate: 0,
        computedAt: DateTime.now(),
      );
    }
  }

  // ── 2. LIVE STREAM: Active urgent donations ───────────────────────────────

  static Stream<int> streamUrgentCount() {
    return _db
        .collection('posts')
        .where('status', whereIn: ['active', 'claimed'])
        .where('aiUrgencyLevel', whereIn: ['urgent', 'critical'])
        .snapshots()
        .map((s) => s.docs.length);
  }

  // ── 3. PREDICTIVE ANALYTICS ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Generates AI insights from historical Firestore data.
  /// Returns a list of [PredictiveInsight] cards for the dashboard.
  static Future<List<PredictiveInsight>> getPredictiveInsights() async {
    try {
      final allPostsSnap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('aiScanEnabled', isEqualTo: true)
          .limit(200)
          .get();

      final docs = allPostsSnap.docs;
      if (docs.isEmpty) return _emptyInsights();

      // Category analysis
      final Map<String, int> catCount = {};
      final Map<String, int> catWasted = {};
      final Map<int, int> hourCount = {}; // donation hour → count
      int totalUrgent = 0, totalClaimed = 0;
      double totalScore = 0;
      int scoredCount = 0;

      for (final doc in docs) {
        final d = doc.data();
        final cat = d['aiFoodCategory'] as String? ?? 'other';
        final freshness = d['aiFreshnessStatus'] as String? ?? 'moderate';
        final status = d['status'] as String? ?? 'active';
        final urgency = d['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
        final createdAt = d['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
        final score = (d['aiFreshnessScore'] as num?)?.toInt();

        catCount[cat] = (catCount[cat] ?? 0) + 1;
        if (freshness == 'expiringSoon' || freshness == 'unsafe') {
          catWasted[cat] = (catWasted[cat] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
        if (urgency != 'normal') totalUrgent++;
        if (status == 'claimed' || status == 'assigned' || status == 'delivered') {
          totalClaimed++;
        }
        if (score != null) {
          totalScore += score;
          scoredCount++;
        }
        if (createdAt != null) {
          final h = createdAt.toDate().hour;
          hourCount[h] = (hourCount[h] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
      }

      // Most wasted category
      final topWasted = catWasted.isEmpty
          ? 'N/A'
          : (catWasted.entries.toList()
                ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value)))
              .first
              .key;

      // Peak donation hour
      final peakEntry = hourCount.isEmpty
          ? null
          : (hourCount.entries.toList()
                ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value)))
              .first;
      final peakHour = peakEntry?.key;
      final peakLabel = peakHour == null
          ? 'N/A'
          : '${peakHour == 0 ? 12 : peakHour > 12 ? peakHour - 12 : peakHour}'
              '${peakHour < 12 ? 'am' : 'pm'}';

      // Most requested category
      final topCat = catCount.isEmpty
          ? 'N/A'
          : (catCount.entries.toList()
                ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value)))
              .first
              .key;

      // NGO efficiency score
      final efficiency =
          totalUrgent > 0 ? (totalClaimed / docs.length * 100).round() : 0;
      final avgFreshness =
          scoredCount > 0 ? (totalScore / scoredCount).round() : 0;

      return [
        PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'Most Wasted Category',
          value: _catLabel(topWasted),
          trend: '↑',
          insight:
              'Consider partnering with donors in this category to reduce waste.',
        ),
        PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'Peak Donation Time',
          value: peakLabel,
          trend: '→',
          insight: 'Schedule more volunteers during peak hours for faster pickup.',
        ),
        PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'Most Donated Category',
          value: _catLabel(topCat),
          trend: '↑',
          insight: 'High supply — match with receivers who prefer this type.',
        ),
        PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'Claim Rate',
          value: '$efficiency%',
          trend: efficiency >= 70 ? '↑' : '↓',
          insight: efficiency >= 70
              ? 'Excellent NGO responsiveness.'
              : 'Increase volunteer coverage to improve claim rate.',
        ),
        PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'Avg Freshness Score',
          value: '$avgFreshness/100',
          trend: avgFreshness >= 65 ? '↑' : '↓',
          insight: avgFreshness >= 65
              ? 'Good overall food quality from donors.'
              : 'Encourage donors to post earlier for higher quality scores.',
        ),
      ];
    } catch (_) {
      return _emptyInsights();
    }
  }

  static List<PredictiveInsight> _emptyInsights() => [
        const PredictiveInsight(
          label: 'No Data Yet',
          value: '—',
          trend: '→',
          insight: 'Analytics will appear once AI-scanned posts are available.',
        ),
      ];

  static String _catLabel(String cat) {
    const labels = {
      'fruits': 'Fruits',
      'vegetables': 'Vegetables',
      'cookedMeals': 'Cooked Meals',
      'bakery': 'Bakery',
      'dairy': 'Dairy',
      'meat': 'Meat',
      'beverages': 'Beverages',
      'other': 'Other',
    };
    return labels[cat] ?? cat;
  }

  static void clearCache() {
    _cachedSnapshot = null;
    _lastComputed = null;
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: lib\services\ai_priority_engine.dart
================================================================================

/// AiPriorityEngine — Phase 2
///
/// Handles:
///   1. foodQualityScore  (0–100) — composite freshness/image/delivery score
///   2. Dynamic priority recalculation — updates Firestore posts in-place
///   3. Delivery time prediction — predictedDeliveryMinutes + spoilageRisk
///   4. Stale donation detection — "Food Waste Prevention Engine"
library;

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Enums & constants
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

enum SpoilageRisk { low, moderate, high, critical }

/// Priority tiers used for both posts and tasks
enum PostPriorityTier { normal, urgent, critical, expired }

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Engine
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiPriorityEngine {
  static final _db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // ── 1. FOOD QUALITY SCORE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Computes a 0–100 composite quality score from Firestore post data.
  ///
  /// Components:
  ///   • Freshness (AI)       : 40 pts
  ///   • Confidence (AI)      : 20 pts
  ///   • Remaining hours      : 25 pts
  ///   • Moderation passed    : 15 pts
  static int computeFoodQualityScore(Map<String, dynamic> postData) {
    double score = 0;

    // Freshness (40 pts)
    final freshnessStatus = postData['aiFreshnessStatus'] as String? ?? 'moderate';
    score += switch (freshnessStatus) {
      'fresh' => 40.0,
      'moderate' => 28.0,
      'expiringSoon' => 14.0,
      'unsafe' => 0.0,
      _ => 20.0,
    };

    // Confidence (20 pts)
    final confidence = (postData['aiConfidence'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 50.0;
    // confidence is stored as 0–100 int in Firestore
    score += (confidence / 100 * 20).clamp(0, 20);

    // Remaining hours (25 pts — full at 24h+, zero at 0h)
    final remainHours =
        (postData['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
    final hourPts = (remainHours / 24 * 25).clamp(0.0, 25.0);
    score += hourPts;

    // Moderation (15 pts)
    final modPassed = postData['aiModerationPassed'] as bool? ?? true;
    if (modPassed) score += 15;

    return score.round().clamp(0, 100);
  }

  // ── 2. DYNAMIC PRIORITY RECALCULATION ─────────────────────────────────────

  /// Recalculates aiUrgencyLevel and postPriority for all active posts.
  /// Call this periodically (e.g. via cloud function trigger or app foreground).
  ///
  /// Uses elapsed time since post creation and remaining hours to compute
  /// real-time urgency, independent of the original AI scan result.
  static Future<int> recalculateAllPosts() async {
    int updated = 0;
    try {
      final snap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('status', whereIn: ['active', 'claimed'])
          .limit(100) // batch size guard
          .get();

      final batch = _db.batch();

      for (final doc in snap.docs) {
        final d = doc.data();
        final updatedUrgency = _computeCurrentUrgency(d);
        final tier = _priorityTier(d);
        final qualityScore = computeFoodQualityScore(d);

        batch.update(doc.reference, {
          'aiUrgencyLevel': updatedUrgency,
          'postPriority': tier == PostPriorityTier.normal ? 'normal' : 'urgent',
          'foodQualityScore': qualityScore,
          'priorityLastUpdated': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
        updated++;
      }

      await batch.commit();
    } catch (_) {
      // Silently fail — recalculation is non-critical
    }
    return updated;
  }

  /// Compute real-time urgency from remaining hours.
  /// Does NOT call the AI API — pure local calculation.
  static String _computeCurrentUrgency(Map<String, dynamic> postData) {
    final remaining =
        (postData['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 24.0;
    final createdAt = postData['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
    double effectiveRemaining = remaining;

    if (createdAt != null) {
      final elapsed =
          DateTime.now().difference(createdAt.toDate()).inMinutes / 60.0;
      effectiveRemaining = remaining - elapsed;
    }

    if (effectiveRemaining <= 0) return 'expired';
    if (effectiveRemaining <= 2) return 'critical';
    if (effectiveRemaining <= 6) return 'urgent';
    return 'normal';
  }

  static PostPriorityTier _priorityTier(Map<String, dynamic> d) {
    final u = d['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
    return switch (u) {
      'critical' => PostPriorityTier.critical,
      'urgent' => PostPriorityTier.urgent,
      'expired' => PostPriorityTier.expired,
      _ => PostPriorityTier.normal,
    };
  }

  // ── 3. DELIVERY TIME PREDICTION ───────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Predicts pickup/delivery ETAs and spoilage risk during transit.
  ///
  /// [remainingHours] : AI freshness remaining hours
  /// [urgencyLevel]   : 'normal' | 'urgent' | 'critical'
  ///
  /// Returns a map suitable for merging into a task document.
  static Map<String, dynamic> predictDelivery({
    required double remainingHours,
    required String urgencyLevel,
    int baseDeliveryMinutes = 30, // placeholder for real routing
  }) {
    // Apply urgency multiplier (urgent tasks get deprioritised queue = longer)
    final queueMultiplier = urgencyLevel == 'critical'
        ? 0.8 // fast-tracked
        : urgencyLevel == 'urgent'
            ? 0.9
            : 1.0;
    final predictedMinutes =
        (baseDeliveryMinutes * queueMultiplier).round();

    // Spoilage risk: compare predicted delivery time vs remaining hours
    final deliveryHours = predictedMinutes / 60.0;
    final SpoilageRisk risk;
    final double safetyMargin = remainingHours - deliveryHours;

    if (remainingHours <= 0) {
      risk = SpoilageRisk.critical;
    } else if (safetyMargin <= 0.5) {
      risk = SpoilageRisk.critical;
    } else if (safetyMargin <= 2) {
      risk = SpoilageRisk.high;
    } else if (safetyMargin <= 6) {
      risk = SpoilageRisk.moderate;
    } else {
      risk = SpoilageRisk.low;
    }

    return {
      'predictedDeliveryMinutes': predictedMinutes,
      'spoilageRiskDuringDelivery': risk.name,
      'safetyMarginHours': safetyMargin.clamp(-99.0, 99.0),
      // TODO: Replace with real Google Maps Directions API call
      // TODO: Factor in traffic, volunteer location, road network
    };
  }

  // ── 4. STALE / WASTE DETECTION ────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Returns posts that are at risk of being wasted:
  ///   • Created >2h ago with no requests AND urgency is urgent/critical
  ///   • OR estimatedRemainingHours < 3 and no ngoId assigned
  static Future<List<QueryDocumentSnapshot>> detectStaleDonations({
    int lookbackHours = 6,
  }) async {
    try {
      final cutoff = Timestamp.fromDate(
          DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(hours: lookbackHours)));
      final snap = await _db
          .collection('posts')
          .where('status', isEqualTo: 'active')
          .where('createdAt', isLessThan: cutoff)
          .limit(50)
          .get();

      // Filter: no requests filed
      final results = <QueryDocumentSnapshot>[];
      for (final doc in snap.docs) {
        final d = doc.data();
        final urgency = d['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
        final remaining =
            (d['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 99.0;
        final hasNgo = (d['ngoId'] as String? ?? '').isNotEmpty;

        if (!hasNgo && (urgency != 'normal' || remaining < 3)) {
          results.add(doc);
        }
      }
      return results;
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  /// Boost visibility of a stale post by incrementing its priority.
  /// Writes aiUrgencyLevel → 'urgent' if it was 'normal'.
  static Future<void> boostStalePost(String postId) async {
    try {
      final doc = await _db.collection('posts').doc(postId).get();
      if (!doc.exists) return;
      final d = doc.data()!;
      final current = d['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
      final newUrgency = current == 'normal' ? 'urgent' : 'critical';
      await _db.collection('posts').doc(postId).update({
        'aiUrgencyLevel': newUrgency,
        'postPriority': 'urgent',
        'aiWastePrevention': true,
        'wasteBoostedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── 5. FOOD QUALITY SCORE — convenience async version ────────────────────

  /// Reads post data from Firestore and stores foodQualityScore in-place.
  static Future<int?> computeAndStoreQualityScore(String postId) async {
    try {
      final doc = await _db.collection('posts').doc(postId).get();
      if (!doc.exists) return null;
      final score = computeFoodQualityScore(doc.data()!);
      await _db.collection('posts').doc(postId).update({'foodQualityScore': score});
      return score;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: lib\services\ai_recommendation_service.dart
================================================================================

/// AiRecommendationService — Phase 2
///
/// Provides:
///   1. recommendVolunteers()    — ranks volunteers for a task by score
///   2. buildMatchScore()        — receiver ↔ post affinity scoring
///   3. refreshReceiverProfile() — persists preference profile to Firestore
///   4. getSuggestedPosts()      — "Best Match For You" ranked post list
library;

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Data transfer objects
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class VolunteerScore {
  final String volunteerId;
  final String name;
  final double score;            // 0–100 composite
  final int activeTasks;
  final int completedDeliveries;
  final String recommendation;   // human-readable reason

  const VolunteerScore({
    required this.volunteerId,
    required this.name,
    required this.score,
    required this.activeTasks,
    required this.completedDeliveries,
    required this.recommendation,
  });
}

class PostMatchScore {
  final String postId;
  final double score;            // 0–100 match affinity
  final String reason;

  const PostMatchScore({
    required this.postId,
    required this.score,
    required this.reason,
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Service
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiRecommendationService {
  static final _db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // In-memory cache (cleared on logout)
  static final Map<String, List<VolunteerScore>> _volunteerCache = {};
  static final Map<String, List<PostMatchScore>> _matchCache = {};

  // ── 1. VOLUNTEER RECOMMENDATION ──────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Returns top [maxResults] volunteers ranked by composite score for [taskId].
  /// Factors: active task load, completion history, availability status.
  /// [pickupLocation] is a free-text string used for label display only
  /// (real GPS routing is a TODO for production).
  static Future<List<VolunteerScore>> recommendVolunteers({
    required String taskId,
    required String ngoId,
    String pickupLocation = '',
    int maxResults = 5,
  }) async {
    final cacheKey = '${taskId}_$ngoId';
    if (_volunteerCache.containsKey(cacheKey)) return _volunteerCache[cacheKey]!;

    try {
      // Fetch all volunteers
      final vSnap = await _db
          .collection('users')
          .where('role', isEqualTo: 'Volunteer')
          .limit(50)
          .get();

      if (vSnap.docs.isEmpty) return [];

      final scores = <VolunteerScore>[];

      for (final vDoc in vSnap.docs) {
        final vData = vDoc.data();
        final vId = vDoc.id;
        final name = vData['name'] as String? ?? 'Volunteer';
        final status = vData['status'] as String? ?? 'Available';

        // Count active (assigned but not delivered) tasks
        final activeSnap = await _db
            .collection('tasks')
            .where('assignedTo', isEqualTo: vId)
            .where('status', isEqualTo: 'assigned')
            .get();
        final activeTasks = activeSnap.docs.length;

        // Count completed deliveries
        final doneSnap = await _db
            .collection('tasks')
            .where('assignedTo', isEqualTo: vId)
            .where('status', isEqualTo: 'delivered')
            .get();
        final completed = doneSnap.docs.length;

        // Composite score (0–100)
        // Availability: 30 pts (available = 30, busy = 10, offline = 0)
        final availPts = status == 'Available' ? 30.0 : status == 'Busy' ? 10.0 : 0.0;

        // Load balance: 40 pts (0 active = 40, -8 per active task)
        final loadPts = (40.0 - activeTasks * 8).clamp(0.0, 40.0);

        // Experience: 30 pts (10 pts per 5 deliveries, capped at 30)
        final expPts = (completed / 5 * 10).clamp(0.0, 30.0);

        final composite = availPts + loadPts + expPts;

        String reason;
        if (composite >= 70) {
          reason = 'Excellent availability, low workload';
        } else if (composite >= 45) {
          reason = 'Good fit, moderate workload';
        } else {
          reason = 'Available but currently busy';
        }

        scores.add(VolunteerScore(
          volunteerId: vId,
          name: name,
          score: composite,
          activeTasks: activeTasks,
          completedDeliveries: completed,
          recommendation: reason,
        ));
      }

      // Sort descending by score
      scores.sort((a, b) => b.score.compareTo(a.score));
      final result = scores.take(maxResults).toList();
      _volunteerCache[cacheKey] = result;
      return result;
    } catch (e) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  // ── 2. RECEIVER FOOD MATCHING ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Score a list of posts against a receiver's preference profile.
  /// Returns sorted list of [PostMatchScore] (best first).
  static Future<List<PostMatchScore>> getSuggestedPosts({
    required String receiverId,
    required List<QueryDocumentSnapshot> posts,
  }) async {
    final cacheKey = receiverId;
    if (_matchCache.containsKey(cacheKey)) return _matchCache[cacheKey]!;

    try {
      // Load receiver profile
      final profDoc = await _db.collection('receiverProfiles').doc(receiverId).get();
      final profile = profDoc.data() ?? {};

      final preferredCategories =
          List<String>.from(profile['preferredCategories'] ?? []);
      final familySize = (profile['familySize'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1;
      final requestedPostIds =
          List<String>.from(profile['recentRequestIds'] ?? []);

      final scores = <PostMatchScore>[];

      for (final doc in posts) {
        final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
        double score = 0;
        final reasons = <String>[];

        // Category preference: 30 pts
        final cat = (d['category'] as String? ?? '').toLowerCase();
        if (preferredCategories.any((p) => p.toLowerCase() == cat)) {
          score += 30;
          reasons.add('matches your food preference');
        }

        // AI urgency: 25 pts for critical, 15 for urgent
        final urgency = d['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String? ?? 'normal';
        if (urgency == 'critical') {
          score += 25;
          reasons.add('critically expiring');
        } else if (urgency == 'urgent') {
          score += 15;
          reasons.add('expiring soon');
        }

        // Serves family: 25 pts if qty ≥ familySize
        final qty = (d['quantity'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1;
        if (qty >= familySize) {
          score += 25;
          reasons.add('enough for your family');
        }

        // Not already requested: 10 pts
        if (!requestedPostIds.contains(doc.id)) {
          score += 10;
          reasons.add('new for you');
        }

        // Fresh score bonus: up to 10 pts
        final freshnessScore =
            (d['aiFreshnessScore'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 50;
        score += (freshnessScore / 100 * 10);

        final reason = reasons.isEmpty
            ? 'Available nearby'
            : reasons.take(2).join(' · ');

        scores.add(PostMatchScore(
          postId: doc.id,
          score: score.clamp(0, 100),
          reason: reason,
        ));
      }

      scores.sort((a, b) => b.score.compareTo(a.score));
      _matchCache[cacheKey] = scores;
      return scores;
    } catch (e) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  // ── 3. RECEIVER PROFILE MANAGEMENT ───────────────────────────────────────

  /// Persist receiver preference profile to Firestore.
  /// Called from ReceiverProfilePage settings.
  static Future<void> saveReceiverProfile({
    required String receiverId,
    required int familySize,
    required List<String> preferredCategories,
  }) async {
    await _db.collection('receiverProfiles').doc(receiverId).set({
      'receiverId': receiverId,
      'familySize': familySize,
      'preferredCategories': preferredCategories,
      'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
    _matchCache.remove(receiverId); // invalidate cache
  }

  /// Record a request to build preference history.
  static Future<void> recordRequest({
    required String receiverId,
    required String postId,
    required String category,
  }) async {
    await _db.collection('receiverProfiles').doc(receiverId).set({
      'recentRequestIds': FieldValue.arrayUnion([postId]),
      'categoryHistory': FieldValue.arrayUnion([category]),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
    _matchCache.remove(receiverId);
  }

  // ── Cache management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  static void clearCache() {
    _volunteerCache.clear();
    _matchCache.clear();
  }
}

/// Reusable AI Urgency & Freshness Badge Widgets
///
/// Used across ReceiverHomePage, NGODonationsPage, VolunteerAvailableTasksPage.
/// Reads directly from Firestore post/task data maps.
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Colour palette
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiBadgeColors {
  static const fresh = Color(0xFF27AE60);
  static const moderate = Color(0xFFF39C12);
  static const expiringSoon = Color(0xFFE67E22);
  static const unsafe = Color(0xFFE74C3C);

  static Color forStatus(String? status) {
    switch (status) {
      case 'fresh':
        return fresh;
      case 'moderate':
        return moderate;
      case 'expiringSoon':
        return expiringSoon;
      case 'unsafe':
        return unsafe;
      default:
        return Colors.grey;
    }
  }

  static Color forUrgency(String? urgency) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'critical':
        return unsafe;
      case 'urgent':
        return expiringSoon;
      case 'normal':
        return fresh;
      default:
        return Colors.grey.shade400;
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// AI Freshness Status Badge (Green / Yellow / Orange / Red pill)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiFreshnessBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? status;    // 'fresh' | 'moderate' | 'expiringSoon' | 'unsafe'
  final bool compact;

  const AiFreshnessBadge({super.key, required this.status, this.compact = false});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (status == null || status!.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    final color = AiBadgeColors.forStatus(status);
    final label = _label(status!);

    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: compact ? 6 : 8, vertical: compact ? 3 : 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.13),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.5)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, size: compact ? 10 : 12, color: color),
          SizedBox(width: compact ? 3 : 4),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: compact ? 9 : 11,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
              color: color,
              letterSpacing: 0.4,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _label(String status) {
    switch (status) {
      case 'fresh': return 'Fresh';
      case 'moderate': return 'Moderate';
      case 'expiringSoon': return 'Expiring Soon';
      case 'unsafe': return 'Unsafe';
      default: return status;
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// AI Urgency Badge (pulsing for critical/urgent)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiUrgencyBadge extends StatefulWidget {
  final String? urgency;   // 'normal' | 'urgent' | 'critical'
  final bool compact;

  const AiUrgencyBadge({super.key, required this.urgency, this.compact = false});

  @override
  State<AiUrgencyBadge> createState() => _AiUrgencyBadgeState();
}

class _AiUrgencyBadgeState extends State<AiUrgencyBadge>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _ctrl;
  late Animation<double> _pulse;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _ctrl = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900))
      ..repeat(reverse: true);
    _pulse = Tween<double>(begin: 0.6, end: 1.0)
        .animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _ctrl, curve: Curves.easeInOut));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ctrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  bool get _shouldPulse =>
      widget.urgency == 'critical' || widget.urgency == 'urgent';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (widget.urgency == null || widget.urgency == 'normal') {
      return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }

    final color = AiBadgeColors.forUrgency(widget.urgency);
    final label = widget.urgency == 'critical' ? '🔴 CRITICAL' : '🟠 URGENT';

    final badge = Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: widget.compact ? 6 : 10,
        vertical: widget.compact ? 3 : 5,
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.5)),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: widget.compact ? 9 : 11,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
          color: color,
          letterSpacing: 0.5,
        ),
      ),
    );

    if (_shouldPulse) {
      return AnimatedBuilder(
        animation: _pulse,
        builder: (_, child) => Opacity(opacity: _pulse.value, child: child),
        child: badge,
      );
    }
    return badge;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Countdown / Remaining Hours Badge
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiCountdownBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final double? remainingHours;
  final bool compact;

  const AiCountdownBadge({super.key, required this.remainingHours, this.compact = false});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (remainingHours == null || remainingHours! <= 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    Color color;
    String label;

    if (remainingHours! < 2) {
      color = const Color(0xFFE74C3C);
      label = '${(remainingHours! * 60).round()}m left';
    } else if (remainingHours! < 6) {
      color = const Color(0xFFE67E22);
      label = '${remainingHours!.toStringAsFixed(0)}h left';
    } else {
      color = const Color(0xFF27AE60);
      label = '${remainingHours!.toStringAsFixed(0)}h left';
    }

    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: compact ? 5 : 8, vertical: compact ? 2 : 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, size: compact ? 10 : 12, color: color),
          SizedBox(width: compact ? 2 : 4),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: compact ? 9 : 11,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              color: color,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Combined AI Info Row (badge + urgency + countdown in one line)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AiInfoRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> data;
  final bool compact;

  const AiInfoRow({super.key, required this.data, this.compact = false});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final status = data['aiFreshnessStatus'] as String?;
    final urgency = data['aiUrgencyLevel'] as String?;
    final remaining = (data['estimatedRemainingHours'] as num?)?.toDouble();
    final scanEnabled = data['aiScanEnabled'] as bool? ?? false;

    if (!scanEnabled && status == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return Wrap(
      spacing: 6,
      runSpacing: 4,
      children: [
        if (status != null) AiFreshnessBadge(status: status, compact: compact),
        if (urgency != null && urgency != 'normal')
          AiUrgencyBadge(urgency: urgency, compact: compact),
        if (remaining != null && remaining > 0)
          AiCountdownBadge(remainingHours: remaining, compact: compact),
      ],
    );
  }
}
